===== M0239 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.UnionTypeBuilder.addAlternate(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/UnionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 121-214
Documentation:
/**
   * Adds an alternate to the union type under construction. Returns this
   * for easy chaining.
   */
Method code:
121 |   UnionTypeBuilder addAlternate(JSType alternate) {
122 |     // build() returns the bottom type by default, so we can
123 |     // just bail out early here.
124 |     if (alternate.isNoType()) {
125 |       return this;
126 |     }
127 | 
128 |     isAllType = isAllType || alternate.isAllType();
129 | 
130 |     boolean isAlternateUnknown = alternate instanceof UnknownType;
131 |     isNativeUnknownType = isNativeUnknownType || isAlternateUnknown;
132 |     if (isAlternateUnknown) {
133 |       areAllUnknownsChecked = areAllUnknownsChecked &&
134 |           alternate.isCheckedUnknownType();
135 |     }
136 |     if (!isAllType && !isNativeUnknownType) {
137 |       if (alternate.isUnionType()) {
138 |         UnionType union = alternate.toMaybeUnionType();
139 |         for (JSType unionAlt : union.getAlternates()) {
140 |           addAlternate(unionAlt);
141 |         }
142 |       } else {
143 |         if (alternates.size() > maxUnionSize) {
144 |           return this;
145 |         }
146 | 
147 |         // Function types are special, because they have their
148 |         // own bizarre sub-lattice. See the comments on
149 |         // FunctionType#supAndInf helper and above at functionTypePosition.
150 |         if (alternate.isFunctionType() && functionTypePosition != -1) {
151 |           // See the comments on functionTypePosition above.
152 |           FunctionType other =
153 |               alternates.get(functionTypePosition).toMaybeFunctionType();
154 |           FunctionType supremum =
155 |               alternate.toMaybeFunctionType().supAndInfHelper(other, true);
156 |           alternates.set(functionTypePosition, supremum);
157 |           result = null;
158 |           return this;
159 |         }
160 | 
161 |         // Look through the alternates we've got so far,
162 |         // and check if any of them are duplicates of
163 |         // one another.
164 |         int currentIndex = 0;
165 |         Iterator<JSType> it = alternates.iterator();
166 |         while (it.hasNext()) {
167 |           JSType current = it.next();
168 | 
169 |           // Unknown and NoResolved types may just be names that haven't
170 |           // been resolved yet. So keep these in the union, and just use
171 |           // equality checking for simple de-duping.
172 |           if (alternate.isUnknownType() ||
173 |               current.isUnknownType() ||
174 |               alternate.isNoResolvedType() ||
175 |               current.isNoResolvedType() ||
176 |               alternate.hasAnyTemplate() ||
177 |               current.hasAnyTemplate()) {
178 |             if (alternate.isEquivalentTo(current)) {
179 |               // Alternate is unnecessary.
180 |               return this;
181 |             }
182 |           } else {
183 |             if (alternate.isSubtype(current)) {
184 |               // Alternate is unnecessary.
185 |               return this;
186 |             } else if (current.isSubtype(alternate)) {
187 |               // Alternate makes current obsolete
188 |               it.remove();
189 | 
190 |               if (currentIndex == functionTypePosition) {
191 |                 functionTypePosition = -1;
192 |               } else if (currentIndex < functionTypePosition) {
193 |                 functionTypePosition--;
194 |                 currentIndex--;
195 |               }
196 |             }
197 |           }
198 |           currentIndex++;
199 |         }
200 | 
201 |         if (alternate.isFunctionType()) {
202 |           // See the comments on functionTypePosition above.
203 |           Preconditions.checkState(functionTypePosition == -1);
204 |           functionTypePosition = alternates.size();
205 |         }
206 | 
207 |         alternates.add(alternate);
208 |         result = null; // invalidate the memoized result
209 |       }
210 |     } else {
211 |       result = null;
212 |     }
213 |     return this;
214 |   }

===== M0241 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.getConditionExpression(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 1679-1698
Documentation:
/**
   * Gets the condition of an ON_TRUE / ON_FALSE CFG edge.
   * @param n a node with an outgoing conditional CFG edge
   * @return the condition node or null if the condition is not obviously a node
   */
Method code:
1679 |   static Node getConditionExpression(Node n) {
1680 |     switch (n.getType()) {
1681 |       case Token.IF:
1682 |       case Token.WHILE:
1683 |         return n.getFirstChild();
1684 |       case Token.DO:
1685 |         return n.getLastChild();
1686 |       case Token.FOR:
1687 |         switch (n.getChildCount()) {
1688 |           case 3:
1689 |             return null;
1690 |           case 4:
1691 |             return n.getFirstChild().getNext();
1692 |         }
1693 |         throw new IllegalArgumentException("malformed 'for' statement " + n);
1694 |       case Token.CASE:
1695 |         return null;
1696 |     }
1697 |     throw new IllegalArgumentException(n + " does not have a condition.");
1698 |   }

/**
   * Suppose X is an object with inferred properties.
   * Suppose also that X is used in a way where it would only type-check
   * correctly if some of those properties are widened.
   * Then we should be polite and automatically widen X's properties for him.
   *
   * For a concrete example, consider:
   * param x {{prop: (number|undefined)}}
   * function f(x) {}
   * f({});
   *
   * If we give the anonymous object an inferred property of (number|undefined),
   * then this code will type-check appropriately.
   */
Method code:
1169 |   private void inferPropertyTypesToMatchConstraint(
1170 |       JSType type, JSType constraint) {
1171 |     if (type == null || constraint == null) {
1172 |       return;
1173 |     }
1174 | 
1175 |     ObjectType constraintObj =
1176 |         ObjectType.cast(constraint.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined());
1177 |     if (constraintObj != null) {
1178 |       type.matchConstraint(constraintObj);
1179 |     }
1180 |   }

===== M0246 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.AbstractScopeBuilder.defineSlot(Node,Node,String,JSType,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 1095-1234
Documentation:
/**
     * Defines a symbol in the current scope.
     *
     * @param n the defining NAME or GETPROP or object literal key node.
     * @param parent the {@code n}'s parent.
     * @param variableName The name that this should be known by.
     * @param type the variable's type. It may be {@code null} if
     *     {@code inferred} is {@code true}.
     * @param inferred Whether the type is inferred or declared.
     */
Method code:
1095 |     void defineSlot(Node n, Node parent, String variableName,
1096 |         JSType type, boolean inferred) {
1097 |       Preconditions.checkArgument(!variableName.isEmpty());
1098 | 
1099 |       boolean isGlobalVar = n.isName() && scope.isGlobal();
1100 |       boolean shouldDeclareOnGlobalThis =
1101 |           isGlobalVar &&
1102 |           (parent.isVar() ||
1103 |            parent.isFunction());
1104 | 
1105 |       // If n is a property, then we should really declare it in the
1106 |       // scope where the root object appears. This helps out people
1107 |       // who declare "global" names in an anonymous namespace.
1108 |       Scope scopeToDeclareIn = scope;
1109 |       if (n.isGetProp() && !scope.isGlobal() &&
1110 |           isQnameRootedInGlobalScope(n)) {
1111 |         Scope globalScope = scope.getGlobalScope();
1112 | 
1113 |         // don't try to declare in the global scope if there's
1114 |         // already a symbol there with this name.
1115 |         if (!globalScope.isDeclared(variableName, false)) {
1116 |           scopeToDeclareIn = scope.getGlobalScope();
1117 |         }
1118 |       }
1119 | 
1120 |       // The input may be null if we are working with a AST snippet. So read
1121 |       // the extern info from the node.
1122 |       boolean isExtern = n.isFromExterns();
1123 |       Var newVar = null;
1124 | 
1125 |       // declared in closest scope?
1126 |       CompilerInput input = compiler.getInput(inputId);
1127 |       if (scopeToDeclareIn.isDeclared(variableName, false)) {
1128 |         Var oldVar = scopeToDeclareIn.getVar(variableName);
1129 |         newVar = validator.expectUndeclaredVariable(
1130 |             sourceName, input, n, parent, oldVar, variableName, type);
1131 |       } else {
1132 |         if (!inferred) {
1133 |           setDeferredType(n, type);
1134 |         }
1135 | 
1136 |         newVar =
1137 |           scopeToDeclareIn.declare(variableName, n, type, input, inferred);
1138 | 
1139 |         if (type instanceof EnumType) {
1140 |           Node initialValue = newVar.getInitialValue();
1141 |           boolean isValidValue = initialValue != null &&
1142 |               (initialValue.isObjectLit() ||
1143 |                initialValue.isQualifiedName());
1144 |           if (!isValidValue) {
1145 |             compiler.report(JSError.make(sourceName, n, ENUM_INITIALIZER));
1146 |           }
1147 |         }
1148 |       }
1149 | 
1150 |       // We need to do some additional work for constructors and interfaces.
1151 |       FunctionType fnType = JSType.toMaybeFunctionType(type);
1152 |       if (fnType != null &&
1153 |           // We don't want to look at empty function types.
1154 |           !type.isEmptyType()) {
1155 |         if ((fnType.isConstructor() || fnType.isInterface()) &&
1156 |             !fnType.equals(getNativeType(U2U_CONSTRUCTOR_TYPE))) {
1157 |           // Declare var.prototype in the scope chain.
1158 |           FunctionType superClassCtor = fnType.getSuperClassConstructor();
1159 |           ObjectType.Property prototypeSlot = fnType.getSlot("prototype");
1160 | 
1161 |           // When we declare the function prototype implicitly, we
1162 |           // want to make sure that the function and its prototype
1163 |           // are declared at the same node. We also want to make sure
1164 |           // that the if a symbol has both a Var and a JSType, they have
1165 |           // the same node.
1166 |           //
1167 |           // This consistency is helpful to users of SymbolTable,
1168 |           // because everything gets declared at the same place.
1169 |           prototypeSlot.setNode(n);
1170 | 
1171 |           String prototypeName = variableName + ".prototype";
1172 | 
1173 |           // There are some rare cases where the prototype will already
1174 |           // be declared. See TypedScopeCreatorTest#testBogusPrototypeInit.
1175 |           // Fortunately, other warnings will complain if this happens.
1176 |           Var prototypeVar = scopeToDeclareIn.getVar(prototypeName);
1177 |           if (prototypeVar != null && prototypeVar.scope == scopeToDeclareIn) {
1178 |             scopeToDeclareIn.undeclare(prototypeVar);
1179 |           }
1180 | 
1181 |           scopeToDeclareIn.declare(prototypeName,
1182 |               n, prototypeSlot.getType(), input,
1183 |               /* declared iff there's an explicit supertype */
1184 |               superClassCtor == null ||
1185 |               superClassCtor.getInstanceType().equals(
1186 |                   getNativeType(OBJECT_TYPE)));
1187 | 
1188 |           // Make sure the variable is initialized to something if
1189 |           // it constructs itself.
1190 |           if (newVar.getInitialValue() == null &&
1191 |               !isExtern &&
1192 |               // We want to make sure that when we declare a new instance
1193 |               // type (with @constructor) that there's actually a ctor for it.
1194 |               // This doesn't apply to structural constructors
1195 |               // (like function(new:Array). Checking the constructed
1196 |               // type against the variable name is a sufficient check for
1197 |               // this.
1198 |               variableName.equals(
1199 |                   fnType.getInstanceType().getReferenceName())) {
1200 |             compiler.report(
1201 |                 JSError.make(sourceName, n,
1202 |                     fnType.isConstructor() ?
1203 |                     CTOR_INITIALIZER : IFACE_INITIALIZER,
1204 |                     variableName));
1205 |           }
1206 |         }
1207 |       }
1208 | 
1209 |       if (shouldDeclareOnGlobalThis) {
1210 |         ObjectType globalThis =
1211 |             typeRegistry.getNativeObjectType(GLOBAL_THIS);
1212 |         if (inferred) {
1213 |           globalThis.defineInferredProperty(variableName,
1214 |               type == null ?
1215 |               getNativeType(JSTypeNative.NO_TYPE) :
1216 |               type,
1217 |               n);
1218 |         } else {
1219 |           globalThis.defineDeclaredProperty(variableName, type, n);
1220 |         }
1221 |       }
1222 | 
1223 |       if (isGlobalVar && "Window".equals(variableName)
1224 |           && type != null
1225 |           && type.isFunctionType()
1226 |           && type.isConstructor()) {
1227 |         FunctionType globalThisCtor =
1228 |             typeRegistry.getNativeObjectType(GLOBAL_THIS).getConstructor();
1229 |         globalThisCtor.getInstanceType().clearCachedValues();
1230 |         globalThisCtor.getPrototype().clearCachedValues();
1231 |         globalThisCtor
1232 |             .setPrototypeBasedOn((type.toMaybeFunctionType()).getInstanceType());
1233 |       }
1234 |     }

===== M0261 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.handleBreak(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 551-599
Documentation:

Method code:
551 |   private void handleBreak(Node node) {
552 |     String label = null;
553 |     // See if it is a break with label.
554 |     if (node.hasChildren()) {
555 |       label = node.getFirstChild().getString();
556 |     }
557 |     Node cur;
558 |     Node previous = null;
559 |     Node lastJump;
560 |     Node parent = node.getParent();
561 |     /*
562 |      * Continuously look up the ancestor tree for the BREAK target or the target
563 |      * with the corresponding label and connect to it. If along the path we
564 |      * discover a FINALLY, we will connect the BREAK to that FINALLY. From then
565 |      * on, we will just record the control flow changes in the finallyMap. This
566 |      * is due to the fact that we need to connect any node that leaves its own
567 |      * FINALLY block to the outer FINALLY or the BREAK's target but those nodes
568 |      * are not known yet due to the way we traverse the nodes.
569 |      */
570 |     for (cur = node, lastJump = node;
571 |         !isBreakTarget(cur, label);
572 |         cur = parent, parent = parent.getParent()) {
573 |       if (cur.isTry() && NodeUtil.hasFinally(cur)
574 |           && cur.getLastChild() != previous) {
575 |         if (lastJump == node) {
576 |           createEdge(lastJump, Branch.UNCOND, computeFallThrough(
577 |               cur.getLastChild()));
578 |         } else {
579 |           finallyMap.put(lastJump, computeFallThrough(cur.getLastChild()));
580 |         }
581 |         lastJump = cur;
582 |       }
583 |       if (parent == null) {
584 |         if (compiler.isIdeMode()) {
585 |           // In IDE mode, we expect that the data flow graph may
586 |           // not be well-formed.
587 |           return;
588 |         } else {
589 |           throw new IllegalStateException("Cannot find break target.");
590 |         }
591 |       }
592 |       previous = cur;
593 |     }
594 |     if (lastJump == node) {
595 |       createEdge(lastJump, Branch.UNCOND, computeFollowNode(cur, this));
596 |     } else {
597 |       finallyMap.put(lastJump, computeFollowNode(cur, this));
598 |     }
599 |   }

56 |   @Override
57 |   public void process(Node externs, Node root) {
58 |     if (checkOnly) {
59 |       normalizeNodeTypes(root);
60 |     } else {
61 |       // Don't perform "PrepareAnnotations" when doing checks as
62 |       // they currently aren't valid during sanity checks.  In particular,
63 |       // they DIRECT_EVAL shouldn't be applied after inlining has been
64 |       // performed.
65 |       if (externs != null) {
66 |         NodeTraversal.traverse(
67 |             compiler, externs, new PrepareAnnotations(compiler));
68 |       }
69 |       if (root != null) {
70 |         NodeTraversal.traverse(
71 |             compiler, root, new PrepareAnnotations(compiler));
72 |       }
73 |     }
74 |   }

1714 |   static boolean isStatementParent(Node parent) {
1715 |     // It is not possible to determine definitely if a node is a statement
1716 |     // or not if it is not part of the AST.  A FUNCTION node can be
1717 |     // either part of an expression or a statement.

Documentation:
/**
   * Expect that the first type can be assigned to a symbol of the second
   * type.
   *
   * @param t The node traversal.
   * @param n The node to issue warnings on.
   * @param rightType The type on the RHS of the assign.
   * @param leftType The type of the symbol on the LHS of the assign.
   * @param msg An extra message for the mismatch warning, if necessary.
   * @return True if the types matched, false otherwise.
   */
Method code:
386 |   boolean expectCanAssignTo(NodeTraversal t, Node n, JSType rightType,
387 |       JSType leftType, String msg) {
388 |     if (!rightType.canAssignTo(leftType)) {
389 |       if (bothIntrinsics(rightType, leftType)) {
390 |         // We have a superior warning for this mistake, which gives you
391 |         // the line numbers of both types.
392 |         registerMismatch(rightType, leftType, null);
393 |       } else {
394 |         mismatch(t, n, msg, rightType, leftType);
395 |       }
396 |       return false;
397 |     }
398 |     return true;
399 |   }

===== M0284 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.computeFallThrough(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 796-810
Documentation:
/**
   * Computes the destination node of n when we want to fallthrough into the
   * subtree of n. We don't always create a CFG edge into n itself because of
   * DOs and FORs.
   */
Method code:
796 |   static Node computeFallThrough(Node n) {
797 |     switch (n.getType()) {
798 |       case Token.DO:
799 |         return computeFallThrough(n.getFirstChild());
800 |       case Token.FOR:
801 |         if (NodeUtil.isForIn(n)) {
802 |           return n.getFirstChild().getNext();
803 |         }
804 |         return computeFallThrough(n.getFirstChild());
805 |       case Token.LABEL:
806 |         return computeFallThrough(n.getLastChild());
807 |       default:
808 |         return n;
809 |     }
810 |   }

458 |   private static List<CompilerInput> getAllInputsFromModules(
459 |       List<JSModule> modules) {
460 |     List<CompilerInput> inputs = Lists.newArrayList();
461 |     Map<String, JSModule> inputMap = Maps.newHashMap();

===== M0305 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.LinkedFlowScope.FlatFlowScopeCache.FlatFlowScopeCache(LinkedFlowScope,LinkedFlowScope)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/LinkedFlowScope.java
Original range: 418-482
Documentation:
// A cache at the join of two scope chains.
Method code:
418 |     FlatFlowScopeCache(LinkedFlowScope joinedScopeA,
419 |         LinkedFlowScope joinedScopeB) {
420 |       linkedEquivalent = null;
421 | 
422 |       // Always prefer the "real" function scope to the faked-out
423 |       // bottom scope.
424 |       functionScope = joinedScopeA.flowsFromBottom() ?
425 |           joinedScopeB.getFunctionScope() : joinedScopeA.getFunctionScope();
426 | 
427 |       Map<String, StaticSlot<JSType>> slotsA = joinedScopeA.allFlowSlots();
428 |       Map<String, StaticSlot<JSType>> slotsB = joinedScopeB.allFlowSlots();
429 | 
430 |       symbols = slotsA;
431 | 
432 |       // There are 5 different join cases:
433 |       // 1) The type is declared in joinedScopeA, not in joinedScopeB,
434 |       //    and not in functionScope. Just use the one in A.
435 |       // 2) The type is declared in joinedScopeB, not in joinedScopeA,
436 |       //    and not in functionScope. Just use the one in B.
437 |       // 3) The type is declared in functionScope and joinedScopeA, but
438 |       //    not in joinedScopeB. Join the two types.
439 |       // 4) The type is declared in functionScope and joinedScopeB, but
440 |       //    not in joinedScopeA. Join the two types.
441 |       // 5) The type is declared in joinedScopeA and joinedScopeB. Join
442 |       //    the two types.
443 |       Set<String> symbolNames = Sets.newHashSet(symbols.keySet());
444 |       symbolNames.addAll(slotsB.keySet());
445 | 
446 |       for (String name : symbolNames) {
447 |         StaticSlot<JSType> slotA = slotsA.get(name);
448 |         StaticSlot<JSType> slotB = slotsB.get(name);
449 | 
450 |         JSType joinedType = null;
451 |         if (slotB == null || slotB.getType() == null) {
452 |           StaticSlot<JSType> fnSlot
453 |               = joinedScopeB.getFunctionScope().getSlot(name);
454 |           JSType fnSlotType = fnSlot == null ? null : fnSlot.getType();
455 |           if (fnSlotType == null) {
456 |             // Case #1 -- already inserted.
457 |           } else {
458 |             // Case #3
459 |             joinedType = slotA.getType().getLeastSupertype(fnSlotType);
460 |           }
461 |         } else if (slotA == null || slotA.getType() == null) {
462 |           StaticSlot<JSType> fnSlot
463 |               = joinedScopeA.getFunctionScope().getSlot(name);
464 |           JSType fnSlotType = fnSlot == null ? null : fnSlot.getType();
465 |           if (fnSlotType == null) {
466 |             // Case #2
467 |             symbols.put(name, slotB);
468 |           } else {
469 |             // Case #4
470 |             joinedType = slotB.getType().getLeastSupertype(fnSlotType);
471 |           }
472 |         } else {
473 |           // Case #5
474 |           joinedType =
475 |               slotA.getType().getLeastSupertype(slotB.getType());
476 |         }
477 | 
478 |         if (joinedType != null) {
479 |           symbols.put(name, new SimpleSlot(name, joinedType, true));
480 |         }
481 |       }
482 |     }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.handleReturn(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 639-666
Documentation:

Method code:
639 |   private void handleReturn(Node node) {
640 |     Node lastJump = null;
641 |     for (Iterator<Node> iter = exceptionHandler.iterator(); iter.hasNext();) {
642 |       Node curHandler = iter.next();
643 |       if (curHandler.isFunction()) {
644 |         break;
645 |       }
646 |       if (NodeUtil.hasFinally(curHandler)) {
647 |         if (lastJump == null) {
648 |           createEdge(node, Branch.UNCOND, curHandler.getLastChild());
649 |         } else {
650 |           finallyMap.put(lastJump,
651 |               computeFallThrough(curHandler.getLastChild()));
652 |         }
653 |         lastJump = curHandler;
654 |       }
655 |     }
656 | 
657 |     if (node.hasChildren()) {
658 |       connectToPossibleExceptionHandler(node, node.getFirstChild());
659 |     }
660 | 
661 |     if (lastJump == null) {
662 |       createEdge(node, Branch.UNCOND, null);
663 |     } else {
664 |       finallyMap.put(lastJump, null);
665 |     }
666 |   }

===== M0308 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.filterNoResolvedType(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 840-866
Documentation:
/**
   * When computing infima, we may get a situation like
   * inf(Type1, Type2)
   * where both types are unresolved, so they're technically
   * subtypes of one another.
   *
   * If this happens, filter them down to NoResolvedType.
   */
Method code:
840 |   static JSType filterNoResolvedType(JSType type) {
841 |     if (type.isNoResolvedType()) {
842 |       // inf(UnresolvedType1, UnresolvedType2) needs to resolve
843 |       // to the base unresolved type, so that the relation is symmetric.
844 |       return type.getNativeType(JSTypeNative.NO_RESOLVED_TYPE);
845 |     } else if (type.isUnionType()) {
846 |       UnionType unionType = type.toMaybeUnionType();
847 |       boolean needsFiltering = false;
848 |       for (JSType alt : unionType.getAlternates()) {
849 |         if (alt.isNoResolvedType()) {
850 |           needsFiltering = true;
851 |           break;
852 |         }
853 |       }
854 | 
855 |       if (needsFiltering) {
856 |         UnionTypeBuilder builder = new UnionTypeBuilder(type.registry);
857 |         for (JSType alt : unionType.getAlternates()) {
858 |           if (!alt.isNoResolvedType()) {
859 |             builder.addAlternate(alt);
860 |           }
861 |         }
862 |         return builder.build();
863 |       }
864 |     }
865 |     return type;
866 |   }

===== M0316 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.handleStmtList(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 484-523
Documentation:

Method code:
484 |   private void handleStmtList(Node node) {
485 |     Node parent = node.getParent();
486 |     // Special case, don't add a block of empty CATCH block to the graph.
487 |     if (node.isBlock() && parent != null &&
488 |         parent.isTry() &&
489 |         NodeUtil.getCatchBlock(parent) == node &&
490 |         !NodeUtil.hasCatchHandler(node)) {
491 |       return;
492 |     }
493 | 
494 |     // A block transfer control to its first child if it is not empty.
495 |     Node child = node.getFirstChild();
496 | 
497 |     // Function declarations are skipped since control doesn't go into that
498 |     // function (unless it is called)
499 |     while (child != null && child.isFunction()) {
500 |       child = child.getNext();
501 |     }
502 | 
503 |     if (child != null) {
504 |       createEdge(node, Branch.UNCOND, computeFallThrough(child));
505 |     } else {
506 |       createEdge(node, Branch.UNCOND, computeFollowNode(node, this));
507 |     }
508 | 
509 |     // Synthetic blocks
510 |     if (parent != null) {
511 |       switch (parent.getType()) {
512 |         case Token.DEFAULT_CASE:
513 |         case Token.CASE:
514 |         case Token.TRY:
515 |           break;
516 |         default:
517 |           if (node.isBlock() && node.isSyntheticBlock()) {
518 |             createEdge(node, Branch.SYN_BLOCK, computeFollowNode(node, this));
519 |           }
520 |           break;
521 |       }
522 |     }
523 |   }

===== M0322 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.visitName(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 1260-1289
Documentation:
/**
   * Visits a NAME node.
   *
   * @param t The node traversal object that supplies context, such as the
   * scope chain to use in name lookups as well as error reporting.
   * @param n The node being visited.
   * @param parent The parent of the node n.
   * @return whether the node is typeable or not
   */
Method code:
1260 |   boolean visitName(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
1261 |     // At this stage, we need to determine whether this is a leaf
1262 |     // node in an expression (which therefore needs to have a type
1263 |     // assigned for it) versus some other decorative node that we
1264 |     // can safely ignore.  Function names, arguments (children of LP nodes) and
1265 |     // variable declarations are ignored.
1266 |     // TODO(user): remove this short-circuiting in favor of a
1267 |     // pre order traversal of the FUNCTION, CATCH, LP and VAR nodes.
1268 |     int parentNodeType = parent.getType();
1269 |     if (parentNodeType == Token.FUNCTION ||
1270 |         parentNodeType == Token.CATCH ||
1271 |         parentNodeType == Token.PARAM_LIST ||
1272 |         parentNodeType == Token.VAR) {
1273 |       return false;
1274 |     }
1275 | 
1276 |     JSType type = n.getJSType();
1277 |     if (type == null) {
1278 |       type = getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
1279 |       Var var = t.getScope().getVar(n.getString());
1280 |       if (var != null) {
1281 |         JSType varType = var.getType();
1282 |         if (varType != null) {
1283 |           type = varType;
1284 |         }
1285 |       }
1286 |     }
1287 |     ensureTyped(t, n, type);
1288 |     return true;
1289 |   }

===== M0323 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CheckMissingReturn.enterScope(NodeTraversal)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CheckMissingReturn.java
Original range: 91-114
Documentation:

Method code:
91 |   @Override
92 |   public void enterScope(NodeTraversal t) {
93 |     JSType returnType = explicitReturnExpected(t.getScopeRoot());
94 |     if (returnType == null) {
95 |       return;
96 |     }
97 | 
98 |     if (fastAllPathsReturnCheck(t.getControlFlowGraph())) {
99 |       return;
100 |     }
101 | 
102 |     CheckPathsBetweenNodes<Node, ControlFlowGraph.Branch> test =

===== M0331 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.LinkedFlowScope.equals(Object)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/LinkedFlowScope.java
Original range: 258-308
Documentation:

Method code:
258 |   @Override
259 |   public boolean equals(Object other) {
260 |     if (other instanceof LinkedFlowScope) {
261 |       LinkedFlowScope that = (LinkedFlowScope) other;
262 |       if (this.optimize() == that.optimize()) {
263 |         return true;
264 |       }
265 | 
266 |       // If two flow scopes are in the same function, then they could have
267 |       // two possible function scopes: the real one and the BOTTOM scope.
268 |       // If they have different function scopes, we *should* iterate through all
269 |       // the variables in each scope and compare. However, 99.9% of the time,
270 |       // they're not equal. And the other .1% of the time, we can pretend
271 |       // they're equal--this just means that data flow analysis will have
272 |       // to propagate the entry lattice a little bit further than it
273 |       // really needs to. Everything will still come out ok.
274 |       if (this.getFunctionScope() != that.getFunctionScope()) {
275 |         return false;
276 |       }
277 | 
278 |       if (cache == that.cache) {
279 |         // If the two flow scopes have the same cache, then we can check
280 |         // equality a lot faster: by just looking at the "dirty" elements
281 |         // in the cache, and comparing them in both scopes.
282 |         for (String name : cache.dirtySymbols) {
283 |           if (diffSlots(getSlot(name), that.getSlot(name))) {
284 |             return false;
285 |           }
286 |         }
287 | 
288 |         return true;
289 |       }
290 | 
291 |       Map<String, StaticSlot<JSType>> myFlowSlots = allFlowSlots();
292 |       Map<String, StaticSlot<JSType>> otherFlowSlots = that.allFlowSlots();
293 | 
294 |       for (StaticSlot<JSType> slot : myFlowSlots.values()) {
295 |         if (diffSlots(slot, otherFlowSlots.get(slot.getName()))) {
296 |           return false;
297 |         }
298 |         otherFlowSlots.remove(slot.getName());
299 |       }
300 |       for (StaticSlot<JSType> slot : otherFlowSlots.values()) {
301 |         if (diffSlots(slot, myFlowSlots.get(slot.getName()))) {
302 |           return false;
303 |         }
304 |       }
305 |       return true;
306 |     }
307 |     return false;
308 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.UnionTypeBuilder.reduceAlternatesWithoutUnion()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/UnionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 221-242
Documentation:
/**
   * Reduce the alternates into a non-union type.
   * If the alternates can't be accurately represented with a non-union
   * type, return null.
   */
Method code:
221 |   private JSType reduceAlternatesWithoutUnion() {
222 |     if (isAllType) {
223 |       return registry.getNativeType(ALL_TYPE);
224 |     } else if (isNativeUnknownType) {
225 |       if (areAllUnknownsChecked) {
226 |         return registry.getNativeType(CHECKED_UNKNOWN_TYPE);
227 |       } else {
228 |         return registry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
229 |       }
230 |     } else {
231 |       int size = alternates.size();
232 |       if (size > maxUnionSize) {
233 |         return registry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
234 |       } else if (size > 1) {
235 |         return null;
236 |       } else if (size == 1) {
237 |         return alternates.iterator().next();
238 |       } else {
239 |         return registry.getNativeType(NO_TYPE);
240 |       }
241 |     }
242 |   }

/**
   * Create and start a tracer.
   * Both type and comment may be null. See class comment for usage.
   *
   * @param type The type for totaling
   * @param comment Comment about this tracer
   */
Method code:
235 |   Tracer(@Nullable String type, @Nullable String comment) {
236 |     this.type = type;
237 |     this.comment = comment == null ? "" : comment;
238 |     startTimeMs = clock.currentTimeMillis();
239 |     startThread = Thread.currentThread();
240 |     if (!extraTracingStatistics.isEmpty()) {
241 |       int size = extraTracingStatistics.size();
242 |       extraTracingValues = new long[size];
243 |       int i = 0;
244 |       for (TracingStatistic tracingStatistic : extraTracingStatistics) {
245 |         extraTracingValues[i] = tracingStatistic.start(startThread);
246 |         i++;
247 |       }
248 |     }
249 | 
250 |     ThreadTrace trace = getThreadTrace();
251 | 
252 |     // Do nothing if the current thread trace wasn't initialized.
253 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
254 |       return;
255 |     }
256 | 
257 |     // Check if we are creating too many Tracers.
258 |     if (trace.events.size() >= MAX_TRACE_SIZE) {
259 |       logger.log(Level.WARNING,
260 |                   "Giant thread trace. Too many Tracers created. "
261 |                     + "Clearing to avoid memory leak.",
262 |                   new Throwable(trace.toString()));
263 |       trace.truncateEvents();
264 |     }
265 | 
266 |     // Check if we forgot to close the Tracers.
267 |     if (trace.outstandingEvents.size() >= MAX_TRACE_SIZE) {
268 |       logger.log(Level.WARNING,
269 |                   "Too many outstanding Tracers. Tracer.stop() is missing "
270 |                     + "or Tracer.stop() is not wrapped in a "
271 |                     + "try/finally block. "
272 |                     + "Clearing to avoid memory leak.",
273 |                   new Throwable(trace.toString()));
274 |       trace.truncateOutstandingEvents();
275 |     }
276 | 
277 |     trace.startEvent(this);
278 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionTypeBuilder.inferFromOverriddenFunction(FunctionType,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 243-301
Documentation:
/**
   * Infer the parameter and return types of a function from
   * the parameter and return types of the function it is overriding.
   *
   * @param oldType The function being overridden. Does nothing if this is null.
   * @param paramsParent The LP node of the function that we're assigning to.
   *     If null, that just means we're not initializing this to a function
   *     literal.
   */
Method code:
243 |   FunctionTypeBuilder inferFromOverriddenFunction(
244 |       @Nullable FunctionType oldType, @Nullable Node paramsParent) {
245 |     if (oldType == null) {
246 |       return this;
247 |     }
248 | 
249 |     returnType = oldType.getReturnType();
250 |     returnTypeInferred = oldType.isReturnTypeInferred();
251 |     if (paramsParent == null) {
252 |       // Not a function literal.
253 |       parametersNode = oldType.getParametersNode();
254 |       if (parametersNode == null) {
255 |         parametersNode = new FunctionParamBuilder(typeRegistry).build();
256 |       }
257 |     } else {
258 |       // We're overriding with a function literal. Apply type information
259 |       // to each parameter of the literal.
260 |       FunctionParamBuilder paramBuilder =
261 |           new FunctionParamBuilder(typeRegistry);
262 |       Iterator<Node> oldParams = oldType.getParameters().iterator();
263 |       boolean warnedAboutArgList = false;
264 |       boolean oldParamsListHitOptArgs = false;
265 |       for (Node currentParam = paramsParent.getFirstChild();
266 |            currentParam != null; currentParam = currentParam.getNext()) {
267 |         if (oldParams.hasNext()) {
268 |           Node oldParam = oldParams.next();
269 |           Node newParam = paramBuilder.newParameterFromNode(oldParam);
270 | 
271 |           oldParamsListHitOptArgs = oldParamsListHitOptArgs ||
272 |               oldParam.isVarArgs() ||
273 |               oldParam.isOptionalArg();
274 | 
275 |           // The subclass method might write its var_args as individual
276 |           // arguments.
277 |           if (currentParam.getNext() != null && newParam.isVarArgs()) {
278 |             newParam.setVarArgs(false);
279 |             newParam.setOptionalArg(true);
280 |           }
281 |         } else {
282 |           warnedAboutArgList |= addParameter(
283 |               paramBuilder,
284 |               typeRegistry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE),
285 |               warnedAboutArgList,
286 |               codingConvention.isOptionalParameter(currentParam) ||
287 |                   oldParamsListHitOptArgs,
288 |               codingConvention.isVarArgsParameter(currentParam));
289 |         }
290 |       }
291 | 
292 |       // Clone any remaining params that aren't in the function literal,
293 |       // but make them optional.
294 |       while (oldParams.hasNext()) {
295 |         paramBuilder.newOptionalParameterFromNode(oldParams.next());
296 |       }
297 | 
298 |       parametersNode = paramBuilder.build();
299 |     }
300 |     return this;
301 |   }

/**
   * Determines if the subtree might throw an exception.
   */
Method code:
910 |   public static boolean mayThrowException(Node n) {
911 |     switch (n.getType()) {
912 |       case Token.CALL:
913 |       case Token.GETPROP:
914 |       case Token.GETELEM:
915 |       case Token.THROW:
916 |       case Token.NEW:
917 |       case Token.ASSIGN:
918 |       case Token.INC:
919 |       case Token.DEC:
920 |       case Token.INSTANCEOF:

922 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
923 |         return false;
924 |     }
925 |     for (Node c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
926 |       if (!ControlFlowGraph.isEnteringNewCfgNode(c) && mayThrowException(c)) {
927 |         return true;
928 |       }
929 |     }
930 |     return false;
931 |   }

===== M0374 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.isSubtypeHelper(JSType,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 1076-1104
Documentation:
/**
   * A generic implementation meant to be used as a helper for common subtyping
   * cases.
   */
Method code:
1076 |   static boolean isSubtypeHelper(JSType thisType, JSType thatType) {
1077 |     // unknown
1078 |     if (thatType.isUnknownType()) {
1079 |       return true;
1080 |     }
1081 |     // equality
1082 |     if (thisType.isEquivalentTo(thatType)) {
1083 |       return true;
1084 |     }
1085 |     // all type
1086 |     if (thatType.isAllType()) {
1087 |       return true;
1088 |     }
1089 |     // unions
1090 |     if (thatType.isUnionType()) {
1091 |       UnionType union = thatType.toMaybeUnionType();
1092 |       for (JSType element : union.alternates) {
1093 |         if (thisType.isSubtype(element)) {
1094 |           return true;
1095 |         }
1096 |       }
1097 |     }
1098 |     // proxy types
1099 |     if (thatType instanceof ProxyObjectType) {
1100 |       return thisType.isSubtype(
1101 |           ((ProxyObjectType) thatType).getReferencedTypeInternal());
1102 |     }
1103 |     return false;
1104 |   }

613 |     if (last == null) {
614 |       first = last = child;
615 |       return;
616 |     }
617 |     last.next = child;
618 |     last = child;
619 |   }

===== M0397 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.mergeLineCharNo(int,int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1195-1203
Documentation:
/**
   * Merges the line number and character number in one integer. The Character
   * number takes the first 12 bits and the line number takes the rest. If
   * the character number is greater than <code>2<sup>12</sup>-1</code> it is
   * adjusted to <code>2<sup>12</sup>-1</code>.
   */
Method code:
1195 |   protected static int mergeLineCharNo(int lineno, int charno) {
1196 |     if (lineno < 0 || charno < 0) {
1197 |       return -1;
1198 |     } else if ((charno & ~COLUMN_MASK) != 0) {
1199 |       return lineno << COLUMN_BITS | COLUMN_MASK;
1200 |     } else {
1201 |       return lineno << COLUMN_BITS | (charno & COLUMN_MASK);
1202 |     }
1203 |   }

Documentation:
/**
   * Determines if the given scope should explicitly return. All functions
   * with non-void or non-unknown return types must have explicit returns.
   * @return If a return type is expected, returns it. Otherwise, returns null.
   */
Method code:
150 |   private JSType explicitReturnExpected(Node scope) {
151 |     FunctionType scopeType = JSType.toMaybeFunctionType(scope.getJSType());
152 | 
153 |     if (scopeType == null) {
154 |       return null;
155 |     }
156 |

158 |       return null;
159 |     }
160 |

162 | 
163 |     if (returnType == null) {
164 |       return null;
165 |     }
166 | 
167 |     if (!isVoidOrUnknown(returnType)) {
168 |       return returnType;
169 |     }
170 | 
171 |     return null;
172 |   }

/** Handle bleeding functions and function parameters. */
Method code:
1896 |     private void handleFunctionInputs(Node fnNode) {
1897 |       // Handle bleeding functions.
1898 |       Node fnNameNode = fnNode.getFirstChild();
1899 |       String fnName = fnNameNode.getString();
1900 |       if (!fnName.isEmpty()) {
1901 |         Scope.Var fnVar = scope.getVar(fnName);
1902 |         if (fnVar == null ||
1903 |             // Make sure we're not touching a native function. Native
1904 |             // functions aren't bleeding, but may not have a declaration
1905 |             // node.

1907 |                 // Make sure that the function is actually bleeding by checking
1908 |                 // if has already been declared.

1911 |         }

===== M0424 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverseBranch(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 476-505
Documentation:
/**
   * Traverses a branch.
   */
Method code:
476 |   @SuppressWarnings("fallthrough")
477 |   private void traverseBranch(Node n, Node parent) {
478 |     int type = n.getType();
479 |     if (type == Token.SCRIPT) {
480 |       inputId = n.getInputId();
481 |       sourceName = getSourceName(n);
482 |     }
483 | 
484 |     curNode = n;
485 |     if (!callback.shouldTraverse(this, n, parent)) return;
486 | 
487 |     switch (type) {
488 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
489 |         traverseFunction(n, parent);
490 |         break;
491 | 
492 |       default:
493 |         for (Node child = n.getFirstChild(); child != null; ) {
494 |           // child could be replaced, in which case our child node
495 |           // would no longer point to the true next
496 |           Node next = child.getNext();
497 |           traverseBranch(child, n);
498 |           child = next;
499 |         }
500 |         break;
501 |     }
502 | 
503 |     curNode = n;
504 |     callback.visit(this, n, parent);
505 |   }

279 |   private boolean isJSDocString(int c) {

===== M0447 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.InferJSDocInfo.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/InferJSDocInfo.java
Original range: 92-189
Documentation:

Method code:
92 |   @Override
93 |   public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
94 |     JSDocInfo docInfo;
95 | 
96 |     switch (n.getType()) {
97 |       // Infer JSDocInfo on types of all type declarations on variables.
98 |       case Token.NAME:
99 |         if (parent == null) {
100 |           return;
101 |         }
102 | 
103 |         // Only allow JSDoc on VARs, function declarations, and assigns.
104 |         if (!parent.isVar() &&
105 |             !NodeUtil.isFunctionDeclaration(parent) &&
106 |             !(parent.isAssign() &&
107 |               n == parent.getFirstChild())) {
108 |           return;
109 |         }
110 | 
111 |         // There are four places the doc info could live.
112 |         // 1) A FUNCTION node.
113 |         // /** ... */ function f() { ... }
114 |         // 2) An ASSIGN parent.
115 |         // /** ... */ x = function () { ... }
116 |         // 3) A NAME parent.
117 |         // var x, /** ... */ y = function() { ... }
118 |         // 4) A VAR gramps.
119 |         // /** ... */ var x = function() { ... }
120 |         docInfo = n.getJSDocInfo();
121 |         if (docInfo == null &&
122 |             !(parent.isVar() &&
123 |                 !parent.hasOneChild())) {
124 |           docInfo = parent.getJSDocInfo();
125 |         }
126 | 
127 |         // Try to find the type of the NAME.
128 |         JSType varType = n.getJSType();
129 |         if (varType == null && parent.isFunction()) {
130 |           varType = parent.getJSType();
131 |         }
132 | 
133 |         // If we have no type to attach JSDocInfo to, then there's nothing
134 |         // we can do.
135 |         if (varType == null || docInfo == null) {
136 |           return;
137 |         }
138 | 
139 |         // Dereference the type. If the result is not an object, or already
140 |         // has docs attached, then do nothing.
141 |         ObjectType objType = dereferenceToObject(varType);
142 |         if (objType == null || objType.getJSDocInfo() != null) {
143 |           return;
144 |         }
145 | 
146 |         attachJSDocInfoToNominalTypeOrShape(objType, docInfo, n.getString());
147 |         break;
148 | 
149 |       case Token.GETPROP:
150 |         // Infer JSDocInfo on properties.
151 |         // There are two ways to write doc comments on a property.
152 |         //
153 |         // 1)
154 |         // /** @deprecated */
155 |         // obj.prop = ...
156 |         //
157 |         // 2)
158 |         // /** @deprecated */
159 |         // obj.prop;
160 |         if (parent.isExprResult() ||
161 |             (parent.isAssign() &&
162 |              parent.getFirstChild() == n)) {
163 |           docInfo = n.getJSDocInfo();
164 |           if (docInfo == null) {
165 |             docInfo = parent.getJSDocInfo();
166 |           }
167 |           if (docInfo != null) {
168 |             ObjectType lhsType =
169 |                 dereferenceToObject(n.getFirstChild().getJSType());
170 |             if (lhsType != null) {
171 |               // Put the JSDoc in the property slot, if there is one.
172 |               String propName = n.getLastChild().getString();
173 |               if (lhsType.hasOwnProperty(propName)) {
174 |                 lhsType.setPropertyJSDocInfo(propName, docInfo);
175 |               }
176 | 
177 |               // Put the JSDoc in any constructors or function shapes as well.
178 |               ObjectType propType =
179 |                   dereferenceToObject(lhsType.getPropertyType(propName));
180 |               if (propType != null) {
181 |                 attachJSDocInfoToNominalTypeOrShape(
182 |                     propType, docInfo, n.getQualifiedName());
183 |               }
184 |             }
185 |           }
186 |         }
187 |         break;
188 |     }
189 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.InferJSDocInfo.attachJSDocInfoToNominalTypeOrShape(ObjectType,JSDocInfo,String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/InferJSDocInfo.java
Original range: 201-223
Documentation:
/**
   * Handle cases #1 and #3 in the class doc.
   */
Method code:
201 |   private void attachJSDocInfoToNominalTypeOrShape(
202 |       ObjectType objType, JSDocInfo docInfo, @Nullable String qName) {
203 |     if (objType.isConstructor() ||
204 |         objType.isEnumType() ||
205 |         objType.isInterface()) {
206 |       // Named types.
207 |       if (objType.hasReferenceName() &&
208 |           objType.getReferenceName().equals(qName)) {
209 |         objType.setJSDocInfo(docInfo);
210 | 
211 |         if (objType.isConstructor() || objType.isInterface()) {
212 |           JSType.toMaybeFunctionType(objType).getInstanceType().setJSDocInfo(
213 |               docInfo);
214 |         } else if (objType instanceof EnumType) {
215 |           ((EnumType) objType).getElementsType().setJSDocInfo(docInfo);
216 |         }
217 |       }
218 |     } else if (!objType.isNativeObjectType() &&
219 |         objType.isFunctionType()) {
220 |       // Structural functions.
221 |       objType.setJSDocInfo(docInfo);
222 |     }
223 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.matchLabel(Node,String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 894-905
Documentation:
/**
   * Check if label is actually referencing the target control structure. If
   * label is null, it always returns true.
   */
Method code:
894 |   private static boolean matchLabel(Node target, String label) {
895 |     if (label == null) {
896 |       return true;
897 |     }
898 |     while (target.isLabel()) {
899 |       if (target.getFirstChild().getString().equals(label)) {
900 |         return true;
901 |       }
902 |       target = target.getParent();
903 |     }
904 |     return false;
905 |   }

===== M0462 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.initOptions(CompilerOptions)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 246-309
Documentation:
/**
   * Initialize the compiler options. Only necessary if you're not doing
   * a normal compile() job.
   */
Method code:
246 |   public void initOptions(CompilerOptions options) {
247 |     this.options = options;
248 |     if (errorManager == null) {
249 |       if (outStream == null) {
250 |         setErrorManager(
251 |             new LoggerErrorManager(createMessageFormatter(), logger));
252 |       } else {
253 |         PrintStreamErrorManager printer =
254 |             new PrintStreamErrorManager(createMessageFormatter(), outStream);
255 |         printer.setSummaryDetailLevel(options.summaryDetailLevel);
256 |         setErrorManager(printer);
257 |       }
258 |     }
259 | 
260 |     // DiagnosticGroups override the plain checkTypes option.
261 |     if (options.enables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_TYPES)) {
262 |       options.checkTypes = true;
263 |     } else if (options.disables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_TYPES)) {
264 |       options.checkTypes = false;
265 |     } else if (!options.checkTypes) {
266 |       // If DiagnosticGroups did not override the plain checkTypes
267 |       // option, and checkTypes is enabled, then turn off the
268 |       // parser type warnings.
269 |       options.setWarningLevel(
270 |           DiagnosticGroup.forType(
271 |               RhinoErrorReporter.TYPE_PARSE_ERROR),
272 |           CheckLevel.OFF);
273 |     }
274 | 
275 |     if (options.checkGlobalThisLevel.isOn() &&
276 |         !options.disables(DiagnosticGroups.GLOBAL_THIS)) {
277 |       options.setWarningLevel(
278 |           DiagnosticGroups.GLOBAL_THIS,
279 |           options.checkGlobalThisLevel);
280 |     }
281 | 
282 |     if (options.getLanguageIn() == LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT5_STRICT) {
283 |       options.setWarningLevel(
284 |           DiagnosticGroups.ES5_STRICT,
285 |           CheckLevel.ERROR);
286 |     }
287 | 
288 |     // Initialize the warnings guard.
289 |     List<WarningsGuard> guards = Lists.newArrayList();
290 |     guards.add(
291 |         new SuppressDocWarningsGuard(
292 |             getDiagnosticGroups().getRegisteredGroups()));
293 |     guards.add(options.getWarningsGuard());
294 | 
295 |     ComposeWarningsGuard composedGuards = new ComposeWarningsGuard(guards);
296 | 
297 |     // All passes must run the variable check. This synthesizes
298 |     // variables later so that the compiler doesn't crash. It also
299 |     // checks the externs file for validity. If you don't want to warn
300 |     // about missing variable declarations, we shut that specific
301 |     // error off.
302 |     if (!options.checkSymbols &&
303 |         !composedGuards.enables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_VARIABLES)) {
304 |       composedGuards.addGuard(new DiagnosticGroupWarningsGuard(
305 |           DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_VARIABLES, CheckLevel.OFF));
306 |     }
307 | 
308 |     this.warningsGuard = composedGuards;
309 |   }

===== M0473 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.AbstractScopeBuilder.getDeclaredType(String,JSDocInfo,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 1267-1321
Documentation:
/**
     * Look for a type declaration on a property assignment
     * (in an ASSIGN or an object literal key).
     *
     * @param info The doc info for this property.
     * @param lValue The l-value node.
     * @param rValue The node that {@code n} is being initialized to,
     *     or {@code null} if this is a stub declaration.
     */
Method code:
1267 |     private JSType getDeclaredType(String sourceName, JSDocInfo info,
1268 |         Node lValue, @Nullable Node rValue) {
1269 |       if (info != null && info.hasType()) {
1270 |         return getDeclaredTypeInAnnotation(sourceName, lValue, info);
1271 |       } else if (rValue != null && rValue.isFunction() &&
1272 |           shouldUseFunctionLiteralType(
1273 |               JSType.toMaybeFunctionType(rValue.getJSType()), info, lValue)) {
1274 |         return rValue.getJSType();
1275 |       } else if (info != null) {
1276 |         if (info.hasEnumParameterType()) {
1277 |           if (rValue != null && rValue.isObjectLit()) {
1278 |             return rValue.getJSType();
1279 |           } else {
1280 |             return createEnumTypeFromNodes(
1281 |                 rValue, lValue.getQualifiedName(), info, lValue);
1282 |           }
1283 |         } else if (info.isConstructor() || info.isInterface()) {
1284 |           return createFunctionTypeFromNodes(
1285 |               rValue, lValue.getQualifiedName(), info, lValue);
1286 |         } else {
1287 |           // Check if this is constant, and if it has a known type.
1288 |           if (info.isConstant()) {
1289 |             JSType knownType = null;
1290 |             if (rValue != null) {
1291 |               JSDocInfo rValueInfo = rValue.getJSDocInfo();
1292 |               if (rValueInfo != null && rValueInfo.hasType()) {
1293 |                 // If rValue has a type-cast, we use the type in the type-cast.
1294 |                 return rValueInfo.getType().evaluate(scope, typeRegistry);
1295 |               } else if (rValue.getJSType() != null
1296 |                   && !rValue.getJSType().isUnknownType()) {
1297 |                 // If rValue's type was already computed during scope creation,
1298 |                 // then we can safely use that.
1299 |                 return rValue.getJSType();
1300 |               } else if (rValue.isOr()) {
1301 |                 // Check for a very specific JS idiom:
1302 |                 // var x = x || TYPE;
1303 |                 // This is used by Closure's base namespace for esoteric
1304 |                 // reasons.
1305 |                 Node firstClause = rValue.getFirstChild();
1306 |                 Node secondClause = firstClause.getNext();
1307 |                 boolean namesMatch = firstClause.isName()
1308 |                     && lValue.isName()
1309 |                     && firstClause.getString().equals(lValue.getString());
1310 |                 if (namesMatch && secondClause.getJSType() != null
1311 |                     && !secondClause.getJSType().isUnknownType()) {
1312 |                   return secondClause.getJSType();
1313 |                 }
1314 |               }
1315 |             }
1316 |           }
1317 |         }
1318 |       }
1319 | 
1320 |       return getDeclaredTypeInAnnotation(sourceName, lValue, info);
1321 |     }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Tracer.stop(int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Tracer.java
Original range: 407-434
Documentation:
/**
   * Stop the trace.
   * This may only be done once and must be done from the same thread
   * that started it.
   * @param silence_threshold Traces for time less than silence_threshold
   * ms will be left out of the trace report. A value of -1 indicates
   * that the current ThreadTrace silence_threshold should be used.
   * @return The time that this trace actually ran
   */
Method code:
407 |   long stop(int silence_threshold) {
408 |     Preconditions.checkState(Thread.currentThread() == startThread);
409 | 
410 |     ThreadTrace trace = getThreadTrace();
411 |     // Do nothing if the thread trace was not initialized.
412 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
413 |       return 0;
414 |     }
415 | 
416 |     stopTimeMs = clock.currentTimeMillis();
417 |     if (extraTracingValues != null) {
418 |       // We use extraTracingValues.length rather than
419 |       // extraTracingStatistics.size() because a new statistic may
420 |       // have been added
421 |       for (int i = 0; i < extraTracingValues.length; i++) {
422 |         long value = extraTracingStatistics.get(i).stop(startThread);
423 |         extraTracingValues[i] = value - extraTracingValues[i];
424 |       }
425 |     }
426 | 
427 |     // Do nothing if the thread trace was not initialized.
428 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
429 |       return 0;
430 |     }
431 | 
432 |     trace.endEvent(this, silence_threshold);
433 |     return stopTimeMs - startTimeMs;
434 |   }

===== M0503 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.AbstractScopeBuilder.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 498-555
Documentation:

Method code:
498 |     @Override
499 |     public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
500 |       inputId = t.getInputId();
501 |       attachLiteralTypes(t, n);
502 | 
503 |       switch (n.getType()) {
504 |         case Token.CALL:
505 |           checkForClassDefiningCalls(t, n, parent);
506 |           checkForCallingConventionDefiningCalls(n, delegateCallingConventions);
507 |           break;
508 | 
509 |         case Token.FUNCTION:
510 |           if (t.getInput() == null || !t.getInput().isExtern()) {
511 |             nonExternFunctions.add(n);
512 |           }
513 | 
514 |           // Hoisted functions are handled during pre-traversal.
515 |           if (!NodeUtil.isHoistedFunctionDeclaration(n)) {
516 |             defineFunctionLiteral(n, parent);
517 |           }
518 |           break;
519 | 
520 |         case Token.ASSIGN:
521 |           // Handle initialization of properties.
522 |           Node firstChild = n.getFirstChild();
523 |           if (firstChild.isGetProp() &&
524 |               firstChild.isQualifiedName()) {
525 |             maybeDeclareQualifiedName(t, n.getJSDocInfo(),
526 |                 firstChild, n, firstChild.getNext());
527 |           }
528 |           break;
529 | 
530 |         case Token.CATCH:
531 |           defineCatch(n, parent);
532 |           break;
533 | 
534 |         case Token.VAR:
535 |           defineVar(n, parent);
536 |           break;
537 | 
538 |         case Token.GETPROP:
539 |           // Handle stubbed properties.
540 |           if (parent.isExprResult() &&
541 |               n.isQualifiedName()) {
542 |             maybeDeclareQualifiedName(t, n.getJSDocInfo(), n, parent, null);
543 |           }
544 |           break;
545 |       }
546 | 
547 |       // Analyze any @lends object literals in this statement.
548 |       if (n.getParent() != null && NodeUtil.isStatement(n) &&
549 |           lentObjectLiterals != null) {
550 |         for (Node objLit : lentObjectLiterals) {
551 |           defineObjectLiteral(objLit);
552 |         }
553 |         lentObjectLiterals.clear();
554 |       }
555 |     }

===== M0504 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CheckMissingReturn.anonymous_L57.apply(DiGraphEdge<Node, ControlFlowGraph.Branch>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CheckMissingReturn.java
Original range: 58-79
Documentation:

Method code:
58 |     @Override
59 |     public boolean apply(DiGraphEdge<Node, ControlFlowGraph.Branch> input) {
60 |       // First skill all exceptions.
61 |       Branch branch = input.getValue();
62 |       if (branch == Branch.ON_EX) {
63 |         return false;
64 |       } else if (branch.isConditional()) {
65 |         Node condition = NodeUtil.getConditionExpression(
66 |             input.getSource().getValue());
67 |         // TODO(user): We CAN make this bit smarter just looking at
68 |         // constants. We DO have a full blown ReverseAbstractInterupter and
69 |         // type system that can evaluate some impressions' boolean value but
70 |         // for now we will keep this pass lightweight.
71 |         if (condition != null) {
72 |           TernaryValue val = NodeUtil.getImpureBooleanValue(condition);
73 |           if (val != TernaryValue.UNKNOWN) {
74 |             return val.toBoolean(true) == (Branch.ON_TRUE == branch);
75 |           }
76 |         }
77 |       }
78 |       return true;
79 |     }

===== M0505 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.SuppressDocWarningsGuard.level(JSError)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/SuppressDocWarningsGuard.java
Original range: 51-93
Documentation:

Method code:
51 |   @Override
52 |   public CheckLevel level(JSError error) {
53 |     Node node = error.node;
54 |     if (node != null) {
55 |       for (Node current = node;
56 |            current != null;
57 |            current = current.getParent()) {
58 |         int type = current.getType();
59 |         JSDocInfo info = null;
60 | 
61 |         // We only care about function annotations at the FUNCTION and SCRIPT
62 |         // level. Otherwise, the @suppress annotation has an implicit
63 |         // dependency on the exact structure of our AST, and that seems like
64 |         // a bad idea.
65 |         if (type == Token.FUNCTION) {
66 |           info = NodeUtil.getFunctionJSDocInfo(current);
67 |         } else if (type == Token.SCRIPT) {
68 |           info = current.getJSDocInfo();
69 |         } else if (type == Token.ASSIGN) {
70 |           Node rhs = current.getLastChild();
71 |           if (rhs.isFunction()) {
72 |             info = NodeUtil.getFunctionJSDocInfo(rhs);
73 |           }
74 |         }
75 | 
76 |         if (info != null) {
77 |           for (String suppressor : info.getSuppressions()) {
78 |             WarningsGuard guard = suppressors.get(suppressor);
79 | 
80 |             // Some @suppress tags are for other tools, and
81 |             // may not have a warnings guard.
82 |             if (guard != null) {
83 |               CheckLevel newLevel = guard.level(error);
84 |               if (newLevel != null) {
85 |                 return newLevel;
86 |               }
87 |             }
88 |           }
89 |         }
90 |       }
91 |     }
92 |     return null;
93 |   }

744 |     Node processLabeledStatement(LabeledStatement statementNode) {
745 |       Node node = newNode(Token.LABEL);
746 |       Node prev = null;
747 |       Node cur = node;

===== M0517 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 1910-1912
Documentation:
/**
   * Is a FUNCTION node an function expression? An function expression is one
   * that has either no name or a name that is not added to the current scope.
   *
   * <p>Some examples of function expressions:
   * <pre>
   * (function () {})
   * (function f() {})()
   * [ function f() {} ]
   * var f = function f() {};
   * for (function f() {};;) {}
   * </pre>
   *
   * <p>Some examples of functions that are <em>not</em> expressions:
   * <pre>
   * function f() {}
   * if (x); else function f() {}
   * for (;;) { function f() {} }
   * </pre>
   *
   * @param n A node
   * @return Whether n is an function used within an expression.
   */
Method code:
1910 |   static boolean isFunctionExpression(Node n) {
1911 |     return n.isFunction() && !isStatement(n);
1912 |   }

===== M0520 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocTokenStream.getChar()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocTokenStream.java
Original range: 336-390
Documentation:

Method code:
336 |   protected int getChar() {
337 |     if (ungetCursor != 0) {
338 |       cursor++;
339 |       --ungetCursor;
340 |       if (charno == -1) {
341 |         charno = getOffset();
342 |       }
343 |       return ungetBuffer[ungetCursor];
344 |     }
345 | 
346 |     for(;;) {
347 |       int c;
348 |       if (sourceCursor == sourceEnd) {
349 |         hitEOF = true;
350 |         if (charno == -1) {
351 |           charno = getOffset();
352 |         }
353 |         return EOF_CHAR;
354 |       }
355 |       cursor++;
356 |       c = sourceString.charAt(sourceCursor++);
357 | 
358 | 
359 |       if (lineEndChar >= 0) {
360 |         if (lineEndChar == '\r' && c == '\n') {
361 |           lineEndChar = '\n';
362 |           continue;
363 |         }
364 |         lineEndChar = -1;
365 |         lineStart = sourceCursor - 1;
366 |         lineno++;
367 |       }
368 | 
369 |       if (c <= 127) {
370 |         if (c == '\n' || c == '\r') {
371 |           lineEndChar = c;
372 |           c = '\n';
373 |         }
374 |       } else {
375 |         if (isJSFormatChar(c)) {
376 |           continue;
377 |         }
378 |         if (ScriptRuntime.isJSLineTerminator(c)) {
379 |           lineEndChar = c;
380 |           c = '\n';
381 |         }
382 |       }
383 | 
384 |       if (charno == -1) {
385 |         charno = getOffset();
386 |       }
387 | 
388 |       return c;
389 |     }
390 |   }

1708 |   public Node cloneTree() {

1710 |     for (Node n2 = getFirstChild(); n2 != null; n2 = n2.getNext()) {

===== M0562 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.DataFlowAnalysis.BranchedForwardDataFlowAnalysis.flow(DiGraphNode<N, Branch>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/DataFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 443-455
Documentation:

Method code:
443 |     @Override
444 |     protected final boolean flow(DiGraphNode<N, Branch> node) {
445 |       BranchedFlowState<L> state = node.getAnnotation();
446 |       List<L> outBefore = state.out;
447 |       state.out = branchedFlowThrough(node.getValue(), state.in);
448 |       Preconditions.checkState(outBefore.size() == state.out.size());
449 |       for (int i = 0; i < outBefore.size(); i++) {
450 |         if (!outBefore.get(i).equals(state.out.get(i))) {
451 |           return true;
452 |         }
453 |       }
454 |       return false;
455 |     }

===== M0566 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ClosureCodingConvention.typeofClassDefiningName(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ClosureCodingConvention.java
Original range: 160-181
Documentation:
/**
   * Determines whether the given node is a class-defining name, like
   * "inherits" or "mixin."
   * @return The type of class-defining name, or null.
   */
Method code:
160 |   private SubclassType typeofClassDefiningName(Node callName) {
161 |     // Check if the method name matches one of the class-defining methods.
162 |     String methodName = null;
163 |     if (callName.isGetProp()) {
164 |       methodName = callName.getLastChild().getString();
165 |     } else if (callName.isName()) {
166 |       String name = callName.getString();
167 |       int dollarIndex = name.lastIndexOf('$');
168 |       if (dollarIndex != -1) {
169 |         methodName = name.substring(dollarIndex + 1);
170 |       }
171 |     }
172 | 
173 |     if (methodName != null) {
174 |       if (methodName.equals("inherits")) {
175 |         return SubclassType.INHERITS;
176 |       } else if (methodName.equals("mixin")) {
177 |         return SubclassType.MIXIN;
178 |       }
179 |     }
180 |     return null;
181 |   }

===== M0567 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.processFunctionNode(FunctionNode)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 626-708
Documentation:

Method code:
626 |     @Override
627 |     Node processFunctionNode(FunctionNode functionNode) {
628 |       Name name = functionNode.getFunctionName();
629 |       Boolean isUnnamedFunction = false;
630 |       if (name == null) {
631 |         int functionType = functionNode.getFunctionType();
632 |         if (functionType != FunctionNode.FUNCTION_EXPRESSION) {
633 |           errorReporter.error(
634 |             "unnamed function statement",
635 |             sourceName,
636 |             functionNode.getLineno(), "", 0);
637 | 
638 |           // Return the bare minimum to put the AST in a valid state.
639 |           return newNode(Token.EXPR_RESULT, Node.newNumber(0));
640 |         }
641 |         name = new Name();
642 |         name.setIdentifier("");
643 |         isUnnamedFunction = true;
644 |       }
645 |       Node node = newNode(Token.FUNCTION);
646 |       Node newName = transform(name);
647 |       if (isUnnamedFunction) {
648 |         // Old Rhino tagged the empty name node with the line number of the
649 |         // declaration.
650 |         newName.setLineno(functionNode.getLineno());
651 |         // TODO(bowdidge) Mark line number of paren correctly.
652 |         // Same problem as below - the left paren might not be on the
653 |         // same line as the function keyword.
654 |         int lpColumn = functionNode.getAbsolutePosition() +
655 |             functionNode.getLp();
656 |         newName.setCharno(position2charno(lpColumn));
657 |         maybeSetLengthFrom(newName, name);
658 |       }
659 | 
660 |       node.addChildToBack(newName);
661 |       Node lp = newNode(Token.PARAM_LIST);
662 |       // The left paren's complicated because it's not represented by an
663 |       // AstNode, so there's nothing that has the actual line number that it
664 |       // appeared on.  We know the paren has to appear on the same line as the
665 |       // function name (or else a semicolon will be inserted.)  If there's no
666 |       // function name, assume the paren was on the same line as the function.
667 |       // TODO(bowdidge): Mark line number of paren correctly.
668 |       Name fnName = functionNode.getFunctionName();
669 |       if (fnName != null) {
670 |         lp.setLineno(fnName.getLineno());
671 |       } else {
672 |         lp.setLineno(functionNode.getLineno());
673 |       }
674 |       int lparenCharno = functionNode.getLp() +
675 |           functionNode.getAbsolutePosition();
676 | 
677 |       lp.setCharno(position2charno(lparenCharno));
678 |       for (AstNode param : functionNode.getParams()) {
679 |         Node paramNode = transform(param);
680 |         // When in ideMode Rhino can generate a param list with only a single
681 |         // ErrorNode. This is transformed into an EMPTY node. Drop this node in
682 |         // ideMode to keep the AST in a valid state.
683 |         if (paramNode.isName()) {
684 |           lp.addChildToBack(paramNode);
685 |         } else {
686 |           // We expect this in ideMode or when there is an error handling
687 |           // destructuring parameter assignments which aren't supported
688 |           // (an error has already been reported).
689 |           Preconditions.checkState(
690 |               config.isIdeMode
691 |               || paramNode.isObjectLit()
692 |               || paramNode.isArrayLit());
693 |         }
694 |       }
695 |       node.addChildToBack(lp);
696 | 
697 |       Node bodyNode = transform(functionNode.getBody());
698 |       if (!bodyNode.isBlock()) {
699 |         // When in ideMode Rhino tries to parse some constructs the compiler
700 |         // doesn't support, repair it here. see Rhino's
701 |         // Parser#parseFunctionBodyExpr.
702 |         Preconditions.checkState(config.isIdeMode);
703 |         bodyNode = IR.block();
704 |       }
705 |       parseDirectives(bodyNode);
706 |       node.addChildToBack(bodyNode);
707 |      return node;
708 |     }

Method code:
424 |   @Override
425 |   public boolean shouldTraverse(
426 |       NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
427 |     checkNoTypeCheckSection(n, true);
428 |     switch (n.getType()) {
429 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
430 |         // normal type checking
431 |         final Scope outerScope = t.getScope();
432 |         final String functionPrivateName = n.getFirstChild().getString();
433 |         if (functionPrivateName != null && functionPrivateName.length() > 0 &&
434 |             outerScope.isDeclared(functionPrivateName, false) &&
435 |             // Ideally, we would want to check whether the type in the scope
436 |             // differs from the type being defined, but then the extern
437 |             // redeclarations of built-in types generates spurious warnings.
438 |             !(outerScope.getVar(
439 |                 functionPrivateName).getType() instanceof FunctionType)) {
440 |           report(t, n, FUNCTION_MASKS_VARIABLE, functionPrivateName);
441 |         }
442 | 
443 |         // TODO(user): Only traverse the function's body. The function's
444 |         // name and arguments are traversed by the scope creator, and ideally
445 |         // should not be traversed by the type checker.
446 |         break;
447 |     }
448 |     return true;
449 |   }

   * It isn't possible to always determine if a detached node is a expression,
   * so make a best guess.
   */
Method code:
534 |   private static boolean mayBeStatement(Node n) {

===== M0597 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.updateScopeForTypeChange(FlowScope,Node,JSType,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 462-505
Documentation:
/**
   * Updates the scope according to the result of a type change, like
   * an assignment or a type cast.
   */
Method code:
462 |   private void updateScopeForTypeChange(
463 |       FlowScope scope, Node left, JSType leftType, JSType resultType) {
464 |     Preconditions.checkNotNull(resultType);
465 |     switch (left.getType()) {
466 |       case Token.NAME:
467 |         String varName = left.getString();
468 |         Var var = syntacticScope.getVar(varName);
469 | 
470 |         // When looking at VAR initializers for declared VARs, we trust
471 |         // the declared type over the type it's being initialized to.
472 |         // This has two purposes:
473 |         // 1) We avoid re-declaring declared variables so that built-in
474 |         //    types defined in externs are not redeclared.
475 |         // 2) When there's a lexical closure like
476 |         //    /** @type {?string} */ var x = null;
477 |         //    function f() { x = 'xyz'; }
478 |         //    the inference will ignore the lexical closure,
479 |         //    which is just wrong. This bug needs to be fixed eventually.
480 |         boolean isVarDeclaration = left.hasChildren();
481 |         if (!isVarDeclaration || var == null || var.isTypeInferred()) {
482 |           redeclareSimpleVar(scope, left, resultType);
483 |         }
484 |         left.setJSType(isVarDeclaration || leftType == null ?
485 |             resultType : null);
486 | 
487 |         if (var != null && var.isTypeInferred()) {
488 |           JSType oldType = var.getType();
489 |           var.setType(oldType == null ?
490 |               resultType : oldType.getLeastSupertype(resultType));
491 |         }
492 |         break;
493 |       case Token.GETPROP:
494 |         String qualifiedName = left.getQualifiedName();
495 |         if (qualifiedName != null) {
496 |           scope.inferQualifiedSlot(left, qualifiedName,
497 |               leftType == null ? getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE) : leftType,
498 |               resultType);
499 |         }
500 | 
501 |         left.setJSType(resultType);
502 |         ensurePropertyDefined(left, resultType);
503 |         break;
504 |     }
505 |   }
