===== M0599 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSTypeRegistry.createFromTypeNodesInternal(Node,String,StaticScope<JSType>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSTypeRegistry.java
Original range: 1491-1645
Documentation:
/** @see #createFromTypeNodes(Node, String, StaticScope) */
Method code:
1491 |   private JSType createFromTypeNodesInternal(Node n, String sourceName,
1492 |       StaticScope<JSType> scope) {
1493 |     switch (n.getType()) {
1494 |       case Token.LC: // Record type.
1495 |         return createRecordTypeFromNodes(
1496 |             n.getFirstChild(), sourceName, scope);
1497 | 
1498 |       case Token.BANG: // Not nullable
1499 |         return createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1500 |             n.getFirstChild(), sourceName, scope)
1501 |             .restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
1502 | 
1503 |       case Token.QMARK: // Nullable or unknown
1504 |         Node firstChild = n.getFirstChild();
1505 |         if (firstChild == null) {
1506 |           return getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
1507 |         }
1508 |         return createDefaultObjectUnion(
1509 |             createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1510 |                 firstChild, sourceName, scope));
1511 | 
1512 |       case Token.EQUALS: // Optional
1513 |         return createOptionalType(
1514 |             createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1515 |                 n.getFirstChild(), sourceName, scope));
1516 | 
1517 |       case Token.ELLIPSIS: // Var args
1518 |         return createOptionalType(
1519 |             createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1520 |                 n.getFirstChild(), sourceName, scope));
1521 | 
1522 |       case Token.STAR: // The AllType
1523 |         return getNativeType(ALL_TYPE);
1524 | 
1525 |       case Token.LB: // Array type
1526 |         // TODO(nicksantos): Enforce membership restrictions on the Array.
1527 |         return getNativeType(ARRAY_TYPE);
1528 | 
1529 |       case Token.PIPE: // Union type
1530 |         UnionTypeBuilder builder = new UnionTypeBuilder(this);
1531 |         for (Node child = n.getFirstChild(); child != null;
1532 |              child = child.getNext()) {
1533 |           builder.addAlternate(
1534 |               createFromTypeNodesInternal(child, sourceName, scope));
1535 |         }
1536 |         return builder.build();
1537 | 
1538 |       case Token.EMPTY: // When the return value of a function is not specified
1539 |         return getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
1540 | 
1541 |       case Token.VOID: // Only allowed in the return value of a function.
1542 |         return getNativeType(VOID_TYPE);
1543 | 
1544 |       case Token.STRING:
1545 |         JSType namedType = getType(scope, n.getString(), sourceName,
1546 |             n.getLineno(), n.getCharno());
1547 |         if (resolveMode != ResolveMode.LAZY_NAMES) {
1548 |           namedType = namedType.resolveInternal(reporter, scope);
1549 |         }
1550 |         if ((namedType instanceof ObjectType) &&
1551 |             !(nonNullableTypeNames.contains(n.getString()))) {
1552 |           Node typeList = n.getFirstChild();
1553 |           if (typeList != null &&
1554 |               ("Array".equals(n.getString()) ||
1555 |                "Object".equals(n.getString()))) {
1556 |             JSType parameterType =
1557 |                 createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1558 |                     typeList.getLastChild(), sourceName, scope);
1559 |             namedType = new ParameterizedType(
1560 |                 this, (ObjectType) namedType, parameterType);
1561 |             if (typeList.hasMoreThanOneChild()) {
1562 |               JSType indexType =
1563 |                   createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1564 |                       typeList.getFirstChild(), sourceName, scope);
1565 |               namedType = new IndexedType(
1566 |                   this, (ObjectType) namedType, indexType);
1567 |             }
1568 |           }
1569 |           return createDefaultObjectUnion(namedType);
1570 |         } else {
1571 |           return namedType;
1572 |         }
1573 | 
1574 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
1575 |         ObjectType thisType = null;
1576 |         boolean isConstructor = false;
1577 |         Node current = n.getFirstChild();
1578 |         if (current.getType() == Token.THIS ||
1579 |             current.getType() == Token.NEW) {
1580 |           Node contextNode = current.getFirstChild();
1581 |           thisType =
1582 |               ObjectType.cast(
1583 |                   createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1584 |                       contextNode, sourceName, scope)
1585 |                   .restrictByNotNullOrUndefined());
1586 |           if (thisType == null) {
1587 |             reporter.warning(
1588 |                 ScriptRuntime.getMessage0(
1589 |                     current.getType() == Token.THIS ?
1590 |                     "msg.jsdoc.function.thisnotobject" :
1591 |                     "msg.jsdoc.function.newnotobject"),
1592 |                 sourceName,
1593 |                 contextNode.getLineno(), contextNode.getCharno());
1594 |           }
1595 | 
1596 |           isConstructor = current.getType() == Token.NEW;
1597 |           current = current.getNext();
1598 |         }
1599 | 
1600 |         FunctionParamBuilder paramBuilder = new FunctionParamBuilder(this);
1601 | 
1602 |         if (current.getType() == Token.PARAM_LIST) {
1603 |           Node args = current.getFirstChild();
1604 |           for (Node arg = current.getFirstChild(); arg != null;
1605 |                arg = arg.getNext()) {
1606 |             if (arg.getType() == Token.ELLIPSIS) {
1607 |               if (arg.getChildCount() == 0) {
1608 |                 paramBuilder.addVarArgs(getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE));
1609 |               } else {
1610 |                 paramBuilder.addVarArgs(
1611 |                     createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1612 |                         arg.getFirstChild(), sourceName, scope));
1613 |               }
1614 |             } else {
1615 |               JSType type = createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1616 |                   arg, sourceName, scope);
1617 |               if (arg.getType() == Token.EQUALS) {
1618 |                 boolean addSuccess = paramBuilder.addOptionalParams(type);
1619 |                 if (!addSuccess) {
1620 |                   reporter.warning(
1621 |                       ScriptRuntime.getMessage0("msg.jsdoc.function.varargs"),
1622 |                       sourceName, arg.getLineno(), arg.getCharno());
1623 |                 }
1624 |               } else {
1625 |                 paramBuilder.addRequiredParams(type);
1626 |               }
1627 |             }
1628 |           }
1629 |           current = current.getNext();
1630 |         }
1631 | 
1632 |         JSType returnType =
1633 |             createFromTypeNodesInternal(current, sourceName, scope);
1634 | 
1635 |         return new FunctionBuilder(this)
1636 |             .withParams(paramBuilder)
1637 |             .withReturnType(returnType)
1638 |             .withTypeOfThis(thisType)
1639 |             .setIsConstructor(isConstructor)
1640 |             .build();
1641 |     }
1642 | 
1643 |     throw new IllegalStateException(
1644 |         "Unexpected node in type expression: " + n.toString());
1645 |   }

===== M0619 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.getLeastSupertype(JSType)

Original range: 745-751
Documentation:
/**
   * Gets the least supertype of {@code this} and {@code that}.
   * The least supertype is the join (&#8744;) or supremum of both types in the
   * type lattice.<p>
   * Examples:
   * <ul>
   * <li>{@code number &#8744; *} = {@code *}</li>
   * <li>{@code number &#8744; Object} = {@code (number, Object)}</li>
   * <li>{@code Number &#8744; Object} = {@code Object}</li>
   * </ul>
   * @return {@code this &#8744; that}
   */
Method code:
745 |   public JSType getLeastSupertype(JSType that) {
746 |     if (that.isUnionType()) {
747 |       // Union types have their own implementation of getLeastSupertype.
748 |       return that.toMaybeUnionType().getLeastSupertype(this);
749 |     }
750 |     return getLeastSupertype(this, that);

===== M0636 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionTypeBuilder.buildAndRegister()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 576-638
Documentation:
/**
   * Builds the function type, and puts it in the registry.
   */
Method code:
576 |   FunctionType buildAndRegister() {
577 |     if (returnType == null) {
578 |       // Infer return types.
579 |       // We need to be extremely conservative about this, because of two
580 |       // competing needs.
581 |       // 1) If we infer the return type of f too widely, then we won't be able
582 |       //    to assign f to other functions.
583 |       // 2) If we infer the return type of f too narrowly, then we won't be
584 |       //    able to override f in subclasses.
585 |       // So we only infer in cases where the user doesn't expect to write
586 |       // @return annotations--when it's very obvious that the function returns
587 |       // nothing.
588 |       if (!contents.mayHaveNonEmptyReturns() &&
589 |           !contents.mayHaveSingleThrow() &&
590 |           !contents.mayBeFromExterns()) {
591 |         returnType = typeRegistry.getNativeType(VOID_TYPE);
592 |         returnTypeInferred = true;
593 |       }
594 |     }
595 | 
596 |     if (returnType == null) {
597 |       returnType = typeRegistry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
598 |     }
599 | 
600 |     if (parametersNode == null) {
601 |       throw new IllegalStateException(
602 |           "All Function types must have params and a return type");
603 |     }
604 | 
605 |     FunctionType fnType;
606 |     if (isConstructor) {
607 |       fnType = getOrCreateConstructor();
608 |     } else if (isInterface) {
609 |       fnType = typeRegistry.createInterfaceType(
610 |           fnName, contents.getSourceNode());
611 |       if (getScopeDeclaredIn().isGlobal() && !fnName.isEmpty()) {
612 |         typeRegistry.declareType(fnName, fnType.getInstanceType());
613 |       }
614 |       maybeSetBaseType(fnType);
615 |     } else {
616 |       fnType = new FunctionBuilder(typeRegistry)
617 |           .withName(fnName)
618 |           .withSourceNode(contents.getSourceNode())
619 |           .withParamsNode(parametersNode)
620 |           .withReturnType(returnType, returnTypeInferred)
621 |           .withTypeOfThis(thisType)
622 |           .withTemplateNames(templateTypeNames)
623 |           .build();
624 |       maybeSetBaseType(fnType);
625 |     }
626 | 
627 |     if (implementedInterfaces != null) {
628 |       fnType.setImplementedInterfaces(implementedInterfaces);
629 |     }
630 | 
631 |     if (extendedInterfaces != null) {
632 |       fnType.setExtendedInterfaces(extendedInterfaces);
633 |     }
634 | 
635 |     typeRegistry.clearTemplateTypeNames();
636 | 
637 |     return fnType;
638 |   }

===== M0642 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.processTryStatement(TryStatement)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 985-1018
Documentation:

Method code:
985 |     @Override
986 |     Node processTryStatement(TryStatement statementNode) {
987 |       Node node = newNode(Token.TRY,
988 |           transformBlock(statementNode.getTryBlock()));
989 |       Node block = newNode(Token.BLOCK);
990 |       boolean lineSet = false;
991 | 
992 |       for (CatchClause cc : statementNode.getCatchClauses()) {
993 |         // Mark the enclosing block at the same line as the first catch
994 |         // clause.
995 |         if (lineSet == false) {
996 |           block.setLineno(cc.getLineno());
997 |           maybeSetLengthFrom(block, cc);
998 |           lineSet = true;
999 |         }
1000 |         block.addChildToBack(transform(cc));
1001 |       }
1002 |       node.addChildToBack(block);
1003 | 
1004 |       AstNode finallyBlock = statementNode.getFinallyBlock();
1005 |       if (finallyBlock != null) {
1006 |         node.addChildToBack(transformBlock(finallyBlock));
1007 |       }
1008 | 
1009 |       // If we didn't set the line on the catch clause, then
1010 |       // we've got an empty catch clause.  Set its line to be the same
1011 |       // as the finally block (to match Old Rhino's behavior.)
1012 |       if ((lineSet == false) && (finallyBlock != null)) {
1013 |         block.setLineno(finallyBlock.getLineno());
1014 |         maybeSetLengthFrom(block, finallyBlock);
1015 |       }
1016 | 
1017 |       return node;
1018 |     }

===== M0643 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverseRoots(List<Node>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java

Documentation:

Method code:
288 |   public void traverseRoots(List<Node> roots) {
289 |     if (roots.isEmpty()) {
290 |       return;
291 |     }
292 | 
293 |     try {
294 |       Node scopeRoot = roots.get(0).getParent();
295 |       Preconditions.checkState(scopeRoot != null);
296 | 
297 |       inputId = NodeUtil.getInputId(scopeRoot);
298 |       sourceName = "";
299 |       curNode = scopeRoot;
300 |       pushScope(scopeRoot);
301 | 
302 |       for (Node root : roots) {
303 |         Preconditions.checkState(root.getParent() == scopeRoot);
304 |         traverseBranch(root, scopeRoot);
305 |       }
306 | 
307 |       popScope();
308 |     } catch (Exception unexpectedException) {
309 |       throwUnexpectedException(unexpectedException);
310 |     }
311 |   }

508 |   private void validateTry(Node n) {
509 |     validateNodeType(Token.TRY, n);

===== M0653 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.isSubtype(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 1051-1053
Documentation:
/**
   * Checks whether {@code this} is a subtype of {@code that}.<p>
   *
   * Subtyping rules:
   * <ul>
   * <li>(unknown) &mdash; every type is a subtype of the Unknown type.</li>
   * <li>(no) &mdash; the No type is a subtype of every type.</li>
   * <li>(no-object) &mdash; the NoObject type is a subtype of every object
   * type (i.e. subtypes of the Object type).</li>
   * <li>(ref) &mdash; a type is a subtype of itself.</li>
   * <li>(union-l) &mdash; A union type is a subtype of a type U if all the
   * union type's constituents are a subtype of U. Formally<br>
   * {@code (T<sub>1</sub>, &hellip;, T<sub>n</sub>) &lt;: U} if and only
   * {@code T<sub>k</sub> &lt;: U} for all {@code k &isin; 1..n}.</li>
   * <li>(union-r) &mdash; A type U is a subtype of a union type if it is a
   * subtype of one of the union type's constituents. Formally<br>
   * {@code U &lt;: (T<sub>1</sub>, &hellip;, T<sub>n</sub>)} if and only
   * if {@code U &lt;: T<sub>k</sub>} for some index {@code k}.</li>
   * <li>(objects) &mdash; an Object {@code O<sub>1</sub>} is a subtype
   * of an object {@code O<sub>2</sub>} if it has more properties
   * than {@code O<sub>2</sub>} and all common properties are
   * pairwise subtypes.</li>
   * </ul>
   *
   * @return {@code this &lt;: that}
   */
Method code:
1051 |   public boolean isSubtype(JSType that) {
1052 |     return isSubtypeHelper(this, that);
1053 |   }

/** Find the best JSDoc for the given node. */
Method code:
3011 |   static JSDocInfo getBestJSDocInfo(Node n) {
3012 |     JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
3013 |     if (info == null) {
3014 |       Node parent = n.getParent();
3015 |       if (parent == null) {
3016 |         return null;
3017 |       }
3018 | 
3019 |       if (parent.isName()) {
3020 |         return getBestJSDocInfo(parent);
3021 |       } else if (parent.isAssign()) {
3022 |         return parent.getJSDocInfo();
3023 |       } else if (isObjectLitKey(parent, parent.getParent())) {
3024 |         return parent.getJSDocInfo();
3025 |       } else if (parent.isFunction()) {
3026 |         return parent.getJSDocInfo();
3027 |       } else if (parent.isVar() && parent.hasOneChild()) {
3028 |         return parent.getJSDocInfo();
3029 |       } else if ((parent.isHook() && parent.getFirstChild() != n) ||
3030 |                  parent.isOr() ||
3031 |                  parent.isAnd() ||
3032 |                  (parent.isComma() && parent.getFirstChild() != n)) {
3033 |         return getBestJSDocInfo(parent);
3034 |       }
3035 |     }
3036 |     return info;
3037 |   }

===== M0668 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.JoinOp.BinaryJoinOp.computeMidPoint(int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/JoinOp.java
Original range: 66-74
Documentation:
/**
     * Finds the midpoint of a list. The function will favor two lists of
     * even length instead of two lists of the same odd length. The list
     * must be at least length two.
     *
     * @param size Size of the list.
     */
Method code:
66 |     static int computeMidPoint(int size) {
67 |       int midpoint = size >>> 1;
68 |       if (size > 4) {
69 |         /* Any list longer than 4 should prefer an even split point
70 |          * over the true midpoint, so that [0,6] splits at 2, not 3. */
71 |         midpoint &= -2; // (0xfffffffe) clears low bit so midpoint is even
72 |       }
73 |       return midpoint;
74 |     }

===== M0669 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.PrototypeObjectType.isSubtype(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/PrototypeObjectType.java
Original range: 443-491
Documentation:

Method code:
443 |   @Override
444 |   public boolean isSubtype(JSType that) {
445 |     if (JSType.isSubtypeHelper(this, that)) {
446 |       return true;
447 |     }
448 | 
449 |     // Union types
450 |     if (that.isUnionType()) {
451 |       // The static {@code JSType.isSubtype} check already decomposed
452 |       // union types, so we don't need to check those again.
453 |       return false;
454 |     }
455 | 
456 |     // record types
457 |     if (that.isRecordType()) {
458 |       return RecordType.isSubtype(this, that.toMaybeRecordType());
459 |     }
460 | 
461 |     // Interfaces
462 |     // Find all the interfaces implemented by this class and compare each one
463 |     // to the interface instance.
464 |     ObjectType thatObj = that.toObjectType();
465 |     ObjectType thatCtor = thatObj == null ? null : thatObj.getConstructor();
466 |     if (thatCtor != null && thatCtor.isInterface()) {
467 |       Iterable<ObjectType> thisInterfaces = getCtorImplementedInterfaces();
468 |       for (ObjectType thisInterface : thisInterfaces) {
469 |         if (thisInterface.isSubtype(that)) {
470 |           return true;
471 |         }
472 |       }
473 |     }
474 | 
475 |     if (getConstructor() != null && getConstructor().isInterface()) {
476 |       for (ObjectType thisInterface : getCtorExtendedInterfaces()) {
477 |         if (thisInterface.isSubtype(that)) {
478 |           return true;
479 |         }
480 |       }
481 |     }
482 | 
483 |     // other prototype based objects
484 |     if (isUnknownType() || implicitPrototypeChainIsUnknown()) {
485 |       // If unsure, say 'yes', to avoid spurious warnings.
486 |       // TODO(user): resolve the prototype chain completely in all cases,
487 |       // to avoid guessing.
488 |       return true;
489 |     }
490 |     return this.isImplicitPrototype(thatObj);
491 |   }

Original range: 421-489

/**
   * Infer the parameter types from the list of argument names and
   * the doc info.
   */
Method code:
421 |   FunctionTypeBuilder inferParameterTypes(@Nullable Node argsParent,
422 |       @Nullable JSDocInfo info) {
423 |     if (argsParent == null) {
424 |       if (info == null) {
425 |         return this;
426 |       } else {
427 |         return inferParameterTypes(info);
428 |       }
429 |     }
430 | 
431 |     // arguments
432 |     Node oldParameterType = null;
433 |     if (parametersNode != null) {
434 |       oldParameterType = parametersNode.getFirstChild();
435 |     }
436 | 
437 |     FunctionParamBuilder builder = new FunctionParamBuilder(typeRegistry);
438 |     boolean warnedAboutArgList = false;
439 |     Set<String> allJsDocParams = (info == null) ?
440 |         Sets.<String>newHashSet() :
441 |         Sets.newHashSet(info.getParameterNames());
442 |     boolean foundTemplateType = false;
443 |     boolean isVarArgs = false;
444 |     for (Node arg : argsParent.children()) {
445 |       String argumentName = arg.getString();
446 |       allJsDocParams.remove(argumentName);
447 | 
448 |       // type from JSDocInfo
449 |       JSType parameterType = null;
450 |       boolean isOptionalParam = isOptionalParameter(arg, info);
451 |       isVarArgs = isVarArgsParameter(arg, info);
452 | 
453 |       if (info != null && info.hasParameterType(argumentName)) {
454 |         parameterType =
455 |             info.getParameterType(argumentName).evaluate(scope, typeRegistry);
456 |       } else if (oldParameterType != null &&
457 |           oldParameterType.getJSType() != null) {
458 |         parameterType = oldParameterType.getJSType();
459 |         isOptionalParam = oldParameterType.isOptionalArg();
460 |         isVarArgs = oldParameterType.isVarArgs();
461 |       } else {
462 |         parameterType = typeRegistry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
463 |       }
464 | 
465 |       warnedAboutArgList |= addParameter(
466 |           builder, parameterType, warnedAboutArgList,
467 |           isOptionalParam,
468 |           isVarArgs);
469 | 
470 |       if (oldParameterType != null) {
471 |         oldParameterType = oldParameterType.getNext();
472 |       }
473 |     }
474 | 
475 |     // Copy over any old parameters that aren't in the param list.
476 |     if (!isVarArgs) {
477 |       while (oldParameterType != null && !isVarArgs) {
478 |         builder.newParameterFromNode(oldParameterType);
479 |         oldParameterType = oldParameterType.getNext();
480 |       }
481 |     }
482 | 
483 |     for (String inexistentName : allJsDocParams) {
484 |       reportWarning(INEXISTANT_PARAM, inexistentName, fnName);
485 |     }
486 | 
487 |     parametersNode = builder.build();
488 |     return this;
489 |   }

===== M0688 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.process(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 148-190
Documentation:

Method code:
148 |   @Override
149 |   public void process(Node externs, Node root) {
150 |     this.root = root;
151 |     astPositionCounter = 0;
152 |     astPosition = Maps.newHashMap();
153 |     nodePriorities = Maps.newHashMap();
154 |     cfg = new AstControlFlowGraph(computeFallThrough(root), nodePriorities,
155 |                                   edgeAnnotations);
156 |     NodeTraversal.traverse(compiler, root, this);
157 |     astPosition.put(null, ++astPositionCounter); // the implicit return is last.
158 | 
159 |     // Now, generate the priority of nodes by doing a depth-first
160 |     // search on the CFG.
161 |     priorityCounter = 0;
162 |     DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> entry = cfg.getEntry();
163 |     prioritizeFromEntryNode(entry);
164 | 
165 |     if (shouldTraverseFunctions) {
166 |       // If we're traversing inner functions, we need to rank the
167 |       // priority of them too.
168 |       for (DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> candidate : cfg.getDirectedGraphNodes()) {
169 |         Node value = candidate.getValue();
170 |         if (value != null && value.isFunction()) {
171 |           Preconditions.checkState(
172 |               !nodePriorities.containsKey(candidate) || candidate == entry);
173 |           prioritizeFromEntryNode(candidate);
174 |         }
175 |       }
176 |     }
177 | 
178 |     // At this point, all reachable nodes have been given a priority, but
179 |     // unreachable nodes have not been given a priority. Put them last.
180 |     // Presumably, it doesn't really matter what priority they get, since
181 |     // this shouldn't happen in real code.
182 |     for (DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> candidate : cfg.getDirectedGraphNodes()) {
183 |       if (!nodePriorities.containsKey(candidate)) {
184 |         nodePriorities.put(candidate, ++priorityCounter);
185 |       }
186 |     }
187 | 
188 |     // Again, the implicit return node is always last.
189 |     nodePriorities.put(cfg.getImplicitReturn(), ++priorityCounter);
190 |   }

/**
   * Look ahead for a type annotation by advancing the character stream.
   * Does not modify the token stream.
   * This is kind of a hack, and is only necessary because we use the token

2350 |     while (true) {

2355 |         matchedLc = true;
2356 |         break;
2357 |       } else {

2361 |     stream.ungetChar(c);
2362 |     return matchedLc;
2363 |   }

===== M0707 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parseTypeName(JsDocToken)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 1766-1801
Documentation:
/**
   * TypeName := NameExpression | NameExpression TypeApplication
   * TypeApplication := '.<' TypeExpressionList '>'
   * TypeExpressionList := TypeExpression // a white lie
   */
Method code:
1766 |   private Node parseTypeName(JsDocToken token) {
1767 |     if (token != JsDocToken.STRING) {
1768 |       return reportGenericTypeSyntaxWarning();
1769 |     }
1770 | 
1771 |     String typeName = stream.getString();
1772 |     int lineno = stream.getLineno();
1773 |     int charno = stream.getCharno();
1774 |     while (match(JsDocToken.EOL) &&
1775 |         typeName.charAt(typeName.length() - 1) == '.') {
1776 |       skipEOLs();
1777 |       if (match(JsDocToken.STRING)) {
1778 |         next();
1779 |         typeName += stream.getString();
1780 |       }
1781 |     }
1782 | 
1783 |     Node typeNameNode = newStringNode(typeName, lineno, charno);
1784 | 
1785 |     if (match(JsDocToken.LT)) {
1786 |       next();
1787 |       skipEOLs();
1788 |       Node memberType = parseTypeExpressionList(next());
1789 |       if (memberType != null) {
1790 |         typeNameNode.addChildToFront(memberType);
1791 | 
1792 |         skipEOLs();
1793 |         if (!match(JsDocToken.GT)) {
1794 |           return reportTypeSyntaxWarning("msg.jsdoc.missing.gt");
1795 |         }
1796 | 
1797 |         next();
1798 |       }
1799 |     }
1800 |     return typeNameNode;
1801 |   }

===== M0708 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.AbstractScopeBuilder.shouldTraverse(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)

Method code:
466 |     @Override
467 |     public final boolean shouldTraverse(NodeTraversal t, Node n,
468 |         Node parent) {
469 |       inputId = t.getInputId();
470 |       if (n.isFunction() ||
471 |           n.isScript()) {
472 |         Preconditions.checkNotNull(inputId);
473 |         sourceName = NodeUtil.getSourceName(n);
474 |       }
475 | 
476 |       // We do want to traverse the name of a named function, but we don't
477 |       // want to traverse the arguments or body.
478 |       boolean descend = parent == null || !parent.isFunction() ||
479 |           n == parent.getFirstChild() || parent == scope.getRootNode();
480 | 
481 |       if (descend) {
482 |         // Handle hoisted functions on pre-order traversal, so that they
483 |         // get hit before other things in the scope.
484 |         if (NodeUtil.isStatementParent(n)) {
485 |           for (Node child = n.getFirstChild();
486 |                child != null;
487 |                child = child.getNext()) {
488 |             if (NodeUtil.isHoistedFunctionDeclaration(child)) {
489 |               defineFunctionLiteral(child, n);
490 |             }
491 |           }
492 |         }
493 |       }
494 | 
495 |       return descend;
496 |     }

===== M0719 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.parseDirectives(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 470-485
Documentation:
/**
     * Parse the directives, encode them in the AST, and remove their nodes.
     *
     * For information on ES5 directives, see section 14.1 of
     * ECMA-262, Edition 5.
     *
     * It would be nice if Rhino would eventually take care of this for
     * us, but right now their directive-processing is a one-off.
     */
Method code:
470 |     private void parseDirectives(Node node) {
471 |       // Remove all the directives, and encode them in the AST.
472 |       Set<String> directives = null;
473 |       while (isDirective(node.getFirstChild())) {
474 |         String directive = node.removeFirstChild().getFirstChild().getString();
475 |         if (directives == null) {
476 |           directives = Sets.newHashSet(directive);
477 |         } else {
478 |           directives.add(directive);
479 |         }
480 |       }
481 | 
482 |       if (directives != null) {
483 |         node.setDirectives(directives);
484 |       }
485 |     }

===== M0735 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.type.SemanticReverseAbstractInterpreter.getPreciserScopeKnowingConditionOutcome(Node,FlowScope,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/type/SemanticReverseAbstractInterpreter.java
Original range: 124-264
Documentation:

Method code:
124 |   @Override
125 |   public FlowScope getPreciserScopeKnowingConditionOutcome(Node condition,
126 |       FlowScope blindScope, boolean outcome) {
127 |     // Check for the typeof operator.
128 |     int operatorToken = condition.getType();
129 |     switch (operatorToken) {
130 |       case Token.EQ:
131 |       case Token.NE:
132 |       case Token.SHEQ:
133 |       case Token.SHNE:
134 |       case Token.CASE:
135 |         Node left;
136 |         Node right;
137 |         if (operatorToken == Token.CASE) {
138 |           left = condition.getParent().getFirstChild(); // the switch condition
139 |           right = condition.getFirstChild();
140 |         } else {
141 |           left = condition.getFirstChild();
142 |           right = condition.getLastChild();
143 |         }
144 | 
145 |         Node typeOfNode = null;
146 |         Node stringNode = null;
147 |         if (left.isTypeOf() && right.isString()) {
148 |           typeOfNode = left;
149 |           stringNode = right;
150 |         } else if (right.isTypeOf() &&
151 |                    left.isString()) {
152 |           typeOfNode = right;
153 |           stringNode = left;
154 |         }
155 |         if (typeOfNode != null && stringNode != null) {
156 |           Node operandNode = typeOfNode.getFirstChild();
157 |           JSType operandType = getTypeIfRefinable(operandNode, blindScope);
158 |           if (operandType != null) {
159 |             boolean resultEqualsValue = operatorToken == Token.EQ ||
160 |                 operatorToken == Token.SHEQ || operatorToken == Token.CASE;
161 |             if (!outcome) {
162 |               resultEqualsValue = !resultEqualsValue;
163 |             }
164 |             return caseTypeOf(operandNode, operandType, stringNode.getString(),
165 |                 resultEqualsValue, blindScope);
166 |           }
167 |         }
168 |     }
169 |     switch (operatorToken) {
170 |       case Token.AND:
171 |         if (outcome) {
172 |           return caseAndOrNotShortCircuiting(condition.getFirstChild(),
173 |               condition.getLastChild(), blindScope, true);
174 |         } else {
175 |           return caseAndOrMaybeShortCircuiting(condition.getFirstChild(),
176 |               condition.getLastChild(), blindScope, true);
177 |         }
178 | 
179 |       case Token.OR:
180 |         if (!outcome) {
181 |           return caseAndOrNotShortCircuiting(condition.getFirstChild(),
182 |               condition.getLastChild(), blindScope, false);
183 |         } else {
184 |           return caseAndOrMaybeShortCircuiting(condition.getFirstChild(),
185 |               condition.getLastChild(), blindScope, false);
186 |         }
187 | 
188 |       case Token.EQ:
189 |         if (outcome) {
190 |           return caseEquality(condition, blindScope, EQ);
191 |         } else {
192 |           return caseEquality(condition, blindScope, NE);
193 |         }
194 | 
195 |       case Token.NE:
196 |         if (outcome) {
197 |           return caseEquality(condition, blindScope, NE);
198 |         } else {
199 |           return caseEquality(condition, blindScope, EQ);
200 |         }
201 | 
202 |       case Token.SHEQ:
203 |         if (outcome) {
204 |           return caseEquality(condition, blindScope, SHEQ);
205 |         } else {
206 |           return caseEquality(condition, blindScope, SHNE);
207 |         }
208 | 
209 |       case Token.SHNE:
210 |         if (outcome) {
211 |           return caseEquality(condition, blindScope, SHNE);
212 |         } else {
213 |           return caseEquality(condition, blindScope, SHEQ);
214 |         }
215 | 
216 |       case Token.NAME:
217 |       case Token.GETPROP:
218 |         return caseNameOrGetProp(condition, blindScope, outcome);
219 | 
220 |       case Token.ASSIGN:
221 |         return firstPreciserScopeKnowingConditionOutcome(
222 |             condition.getFirstChild(),
223 |             firstPreciserScopeKnowingConditionOutcome(
224 |                 condition.getFirstChild().getNext(), blindScope, outcome),
225 |             outcome);
226 | 
227 |       case Token.NOT:
228 |         return firstPreciserScopeKnowingConditionOutcome(
229 |             condition.getFirstChild(), blindScope, !outcome);
230 | 
231 |       case Token.LE:
232 |       case Token.LT:
233 |       case Token.GE:
234 |       case Token.GT:
235 |         if (outcome) {
236 |           return caseEquality(condition, blindScope, INEQ);
237 |         }
238 |         break;
239 | 
240 |       case Token.INSTANCEOF:
241 |         return caseInstanceOf(
242 |             condition.getFirstChild(), condition.getLastChild(), blindScope,
243 |             outcome);
244 | 
245 |       case Token.IN:
246 |         if (outcome && condition.getFirstChild().isString()) {
247 |           return caseIn(condition.getLastChild(),
248 |               condition.getFirstChild().getString(), blindScope);
249 |         }
250 |         break;
251 | 
252 |       case Token.CASE:
253 |         Node left =
254 |             condition.getParent().getFirstChild(); // the switch condition
255 |         Node right = condition.getFirstChild();
256 |         if (outcome) {
257 |           return caseEquality(left, right, blindScope, SHEQ);
258 |         } else {
259 |           return caseEquality(left, right, blindScope, SHNE);
260 |         }
261 |     }
262 |     return nextPreciserScopeKnowingConditionOutcome(
263 |         condition, blindScope, outcome);
264 |   }

===== M0754 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parseTypeExpression(JsDocToken)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 1686-1727
Documentation:
/**
   * TypeExpression := BasicTypeExpression
   *     | '?' BasicTypeExpression
   *     | '!' BasicTypeExpression
   *     | BasicTypeExpression '?'
   *     | BasicTypeExpression '!'
   *     | '?'
   */
Method code:
1686 |   private Node parseTypeExpression(JsDocToken token) {
1687 |     if (token == JsDocToken.QMARK) {
1688 |       // A QMARK could mean that a type is nullable, or that it's unknown.
1689 |       // We use look-ahead 1 to determine whether it's unknown. Otherwise,
1690 |       // we assume it means nullable. There are 5 cases:
1691 |       // {?} - right curly
1692 |       // {?=} - equals
1693 |       // {function(?, number)} - comma
1694 |       // {function(number, ?)} - right paren
1695 |       // {function(number, ...[?])} - right bracket
1696 |       // {function(): ?|number} - pipe
1697 |       // I'm not a big fan of using look-ahead for this, but it makes
1698 |       // the type language a lot nicer.
1699 |       token = next();
1700 |       if (token == JsDocToken.COMMA ||
1701 |           token == JsDocToken.EQUALS ||
1702 |           token == JsDocToken.RB ||
1703 |           token == JsDocToken.RC ||
1704 |           token == JsDocToken.RP ||
1705 |           token == JsDocToken.PIPE) {
1706 |         restoreLookAhead(token);
1707 |         return newNode(Token.QMARK);
1708 |       }
1709 | 
1710 |       return wrapNode(Token.QMARK, parseBasicTypeExpression(token));
1711 |     } else if (token == JsDocToken.BANG) {
1712 |       return wrapNode(Token.BANG, parseBasicTypeExpression(next()));
1713 |     } else {
1714 |       Node basicTypeExpr = parseBasicTypeExpression(token);
1715 |       if (basicTypeExpr != null) {
1716 |         if (match(JsDocToken.QMARK)) {
1717 |           next();
1718 |           return wrapNode(Token.QMARK, basicTypeExpr);
1719 |         } else if (match(JsDocToken.BANG)) {
1720 |           next();
1721 |           return wrapNode(Token.BANG, basicTypeExpr);
1722 |         }
1723 |       }
1724 | 
1725 |       return basicTypeExpr;
1726 |     }
1727 |   }

===== M0771 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocTokenStream.getCharIgnoreLineEnd()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocTokenStream.java
Original range: 392-436
Documentation:

Method code:
392 |   private int getCharIgnoreLineEnd() {
393 |     if (ungetCursor != 0) {
394 |       cursor++;
395 |       --ungetCursor;
396 |       if (charno == -1) {
397 |         charno = getOffset();
398 |       }
399 |       return ungetBuffer[ungetCursor];
400 |     }
401 | 
402 |     for(;;) {
403 |       int c;
404 |       if (sourceCursor == sourceEnd) {
405 |         hitEOF = true;
406 |         if (charno == -1) {
407 |           charno = getOffset();
408 |         }
409 |         return EOF_CHAR;
410 |       }
411 |       cursor++;
412 |       c = sourceString.charAt(sourceCursor++);
413 | 
414 | 
415 |       if (c <= 127) {
416 |         if (c == '\n' || c == '\r') {
417 |           lineEndChar = c;
418 |           c = '\n';
419 |         }
420 |       } else {
421 |         if (isJSFormatChar(c)) {
422 |           continue;
423 |         }
424 |         if (ScriptRuntime.isJSLineTerminator(c)) {
425 |           lineEndChar = c;
426 |           c = '\n';
427 |         }
428 |       }
429 | 
430 |       if (charno == -1) {
431 |         charno = getOffset();
432 |       }
433 | 
434 |       return c;
435 |     }
436 |   }

   * each property separated by dots. If the node ultimately under the left
   * sub-tree is not a simple name, this is not a valid qualified name.
   *
   * @return a null if this is not a qualified name, or a dot-separated string
   *         of the name and properties.
   */
Method code:
1567 |   public String getQualifiedName() {
1568 |     if (type == Token.NAME) {
1569 |       String name = getString();
1570 |       return name.isEmpty() ? null : name;
1571 |     } else if (type == Token.GETPROP) {
1572 |       String left = getFirstChild().getQualifiedName();
1573 |       if (left == null) {
1574 |         return null;
1575 |       }
1576 |       return left + "." + getLastChild().getString();
1577 |     } else if (type == Token.THIS) {
1578 |       return "this";
1579 |     } else {
1580 |       return null;
1581 |     }
1582 |   }

===== M0799 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.visitReturn(NodeTraversal,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 1693-1728
Documentation:
/**
   * Visits a RETURN node.
   *
   * @param t The node traversal object that supplies context, such as the
   * scope chain to use in name lookups as well as error reporting.
   * @param n The node being visited.
   */
Method code:
1693 |   private void visitReturn(NodeTraversal t, Node n) {
1694 |     Node function = t.getEnclosingFunction();
1695 | 
1696 |     // This is a misplaced return, but the real JS will fail to compile,
1697 |     // so let it go.
1698 |     if (function == null) {
1699 |       return;
1700 |     }
1701 |     JSType jsType = getJSType(function);
1702 | 
1703 |     if (jsType.isFunctionType()) {
1704 |       FunctionType functionType = jsType.toMaybeFunctionType();
1705 | 
1706 |       JSType returnType = functionType.getReturnType();
1707 | 
1708 |       // if no return type is specified, undefined must be returned
1709 |       // (it's a void function)
1710 |       if (returnType == null) {
1711 |         returnType = getNativeType(VOID_TYPE);
1712 |       }
1713 | 
1714 |       // fetching the returned value's type
1715 |       Node valueNode = n.getFirstChild();
1716 |       JSType actualReturnType;
1717 |       if (valueNode == null) {
1718 |         actualReturnType = getNativeType(VOID_TYPE);
1719 |         valueNode = n;
1720 |       } else {
1721 |         actualReturnType = getJSType(valueNode);
1722 |       }
1723 | 
1724 |       // verifying
1725 |       validator.expectCanAssignTo(t, valueNode, actualReturnType, returnType,
1726 |           "inconsistent return type");
1727 |     }
1728 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parseBasicTypeExpression(JsDocToken)

Documentation:
/**
   * BasicTypeExpression := '*' | 'null' | 'undefined' | TypeName
   *     | FunctionType | UnionType | RecordType | ArrayType
   */
Method code:
1733 |   private Node parseBasicTypeExpression(JsDocToken token) {
1734 |     if (token == JsDocToken.STAR) {
1735 |       return newNode(Token.STAR);
1736 |     } else if (token == JsDocToken.LB) {
1737 |       skipEOLs();
1738 |       return parseArrayType(next());
1739 |     } else if (token == JsDocToken.LC) {
1740 |       skipEOLs();
1741 |       return parseRecordType(next());
1742 |     } else if (token == JsDocToken.LP) {
1743 |       skipEOLs();
1744 |       return parseUnionType(next());
1745 |     } else if (token == JsDocToken.STRING) {
1746 |       String string = stream.getString();
1747 |       if ("function".equals(string)) {
1748 |         skipEOLs();
1749 |         return parseFunctionType(next());
1750 |       } else if ("null".equals(string) || "undefined".equals(string)) {
1751 |         return newStringNode(string);
1752 |       } else {
1753 |         return parseTypeName(token);
1754 |       }
1755 |     }
1756 | 
1757 |     restoreLookAhead(token);
1758 |     return reportGenericTypeSyntaxWarning();
1759 |   }

===== M0802 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocTokenStream.getJsDocToken()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocTokenStream.java
Original range: 58-194
Documentation:
/**
   * Tokenizes JSDoc comments.
   */
Method code:
58 |   @SuppressWarnings("fallthrough")
59 |   final JsDocToken getJsDocToken() {
60 |     int c;
61 |     stringBufferTop = 0;
62 |     for (;;) {
63 |       // eat white spaces
64 |       for (;;) {
65 |         charno = -1;
66 |         c = getChar();
67 |         if (c == EOF_CHAR) {
68 |           return JsDocToken.EOF;
69 |         } else if (c == '\n') {
70 |           return JsDocToken.EOL;
71 |         } else if (!isJSSpace(c)) {
72 |           break;
73 |         }
74 |       }
75 | 
76 |       switch (c) {
77 |         // annotation, e.g. @type or @constructor
78 |         case '@':
79 |           do {
80 |             c = getChar();
81 |             if (isAlpha(c)) {
82 |               addToString(c);
83 |             } else {
84 |               ungetChar(c);
85 |               this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
86 |               stringBufferTop = 0;
87 |               return JsDocToken.ANNOTATION;
88 |             }
89 |           } while (true);
90 | 
91 |         case '*':
92 |           if (matchChar('/')) {
93 |             return JsDocToken.EOC;
94 |           } else {
95 |             return JsDocToken.STAR;
96 |           }
97 | 
98 |         case ',':
99 |           return JsDocToken.COMMA;
100 | 
101 |         case '>':
102 |           return JsDocToken.GT;
103 | 
104 |         case '(':
105 |           return JsDocToken.LP;
106 | 
107 |         case ')':
108 |           return JsDocToken.RP;
109 | 
110 |         case '{':
111 |           return JsDocToken.LC;
112 | 
113 |         case '}':
114 |           return JsDocToken.RC;
115 | 
116 |         case '[':
117 |           return JsDocToken.LB;
118 | 
119 |         case ']':
120 |           return JsDocToken.RB;
121 | 
122 |         case '?':
123 |           return JsDocToken.QMARK;
124 | 
125 |         case '!':
126 |           return JsDocToken.BANG;
127 | 
128 |         case ':':
129 |           return JsDocToken.COLON;
130 | 
131 |         case '=':
132 |           return JsDocToken.EQUALS;
133 | 
134 |         case '|':
135 |           matchChar('|');
136 |           return JsDocToken.PIPE;
137 | 
138 |         case '.':
139 |           c = getChar();
140 |           if (c == '<') {
141 |             return JsDocToken.LT;
142 |           } else {
143 |             if (c == '.') {
144 |               c = getChar();
145 |               if (c == '.') {
146 |                 return JsDocToken.ELLIPSIS;
147 |               } else {
148 |                 addToString('.');
149 |               }
150 |             }
151 |             // we may backtrack across line boundary
152 |             ungetBuffer[ungetCursor++] = c;
153 |             c = '.';
154 |           }
155 |           // fall through
156 | 
157 |         default: {
158 |           // recognize a JsDoc string but discard last . if it is followed by
159 |           // a non-JsDoc comment char, e.g. Array.<
160 |           int c1 = c;
161 |           addToString(c);
162 |           int c2 = getChar();
163 |           if (!isJSDocString(c2)) {
164 |             ungetChar(c2);
165 |             this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
166 |             stringBufferTop = 0;
167 |             return JsDocToken.STRING;
168 |           } else {
169 |             do {
170 |               c1 = c2;
171 |               c2 = getChar();
172 |               if (c1 == '.' && c2 == '<') {
173 |                 ungetChar(c2);
174 |                 ungetChar(c1);
175 |                 this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
176 |                 stringBufferTop = 0;
177 |                 return JsDocToken.STRING;
178 |               } else {
179 |                 if (isJSDocString(c2)) {
180 |                   addToString(c1);
181 |                 } else {
182 |                   ungetChar(c2);
183 |                   addToString(c1);
184 |                   this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
185 |                   stringBufferTop = 0;
186 |                   return JsDocToken.STRING;
187 |                 }
188 |               }
189 |             } while (true);
190 |           }
191 |         }
192 |       }
193 |     }
194 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionTypeBuilder.inferInheritance(JSDocInfo)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 319-366
Documentation:
/**
   * Infer the role of the function (whether it's a constructor or interface)
   * and what it inherits from in JSDocInfo.
   */
Method code:
319 |   FunctionTypeBuilder inferInheritance(@Nullable JSDocInfo info) {
320 |     if (info != null) {
321 |       isConstructor = info.isConstructor();
322 |       isInterface = info.isInterface();
323 | 
324 |       // base type
325 |       if (info.hasBaseType()) {
326 |         if (isConstructor) {
327 |           JSType maybeBaseType =
328 |               info.getBaseType().evaluate(scope, typeRegistry);
329 |           if (maybeBaseType != null &&
330 |               maybeBaseType.setValidator(new ExtendedTypeValidator())) {
331 |             baseType = (ObjectType) maybeBaseType;
332 |           }
333 |         } else {
334 |           reportWarning(EXTENDS_WITHOUT_TYPEDEF, fnName);
335 |         }
336 |       }
337 | 
338 |       // implemented interfaces
339 |       if (isConstructor || isInterface) {
340 |         implementedInterfaces = Lists.newArrayList();
341 |         for (JSTypeExpression t : info.getImplementedInterfaces()) {
342 |           JSType maybeInterType = t.evaluate(scope, typeRegistry);
343 |           if (maybeInterType != null &&
344 |               maybeInterType.setValidator(new ImplementedTypeValidator())) {
345 |             implementedInterfaces.add((ObjectType) maybeInterType);
346 |           }
347 |         }
348 |       } else if (info.getImplementedInterfaceCount() > 0) {
349 |         reportWarning(IMPLEMENTS_WITHOUT_CONSTRUCTOR, fnName);
350 |       }
351 | 
352 |       // extended interfaces (for interface only)
353 |       if (isInterface) {
354 |         extendedInterfaces = Lists.newArrayList();
355 |         for (JSTypeExpression t : info.getExtendedInterfaces()) {
356 |           JSType maybeInterfaceType = t.evaluate(scope, typeRegistry);
357 |           if (maybeInterfaceType != null &&
358 |               maybeInterfaceType.setValidator(new ExtendedTypeValidator())) {
359 |             extendedInterfaces.add((ObjectType) maybeInterfaceType);
360 |           }
361 |         }
362 |       }
363 |     }
364 | 
365 |     return this;
366 |   }

===== M0812 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.visitCall(NodeTraversal,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 1593-1645
Documentation:
/**
   * Visits a CALL node.
   *
   * @param t The node traversal object that supplies context, such as the
   * scope chain to use in name lookups as well as error reporting.
   * @param n The node being visited.
   */
Method code:
1593 |   private void visitCall(NodeTraversal t, Node n) {
1594 |     Node child = n.getFirstChild();
1595 |     JSType childType = getJSType(child).restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
1596 | 
1597 |     if (!childType.canBeCalled()) {
1598 |       report(t, n, NOT_CALLABLE, childType.toString());
1599 |       ensureTyped(t, n);
1600 |       return;
1601 |     }
1602 | 
1603 |     // A couple of types can be called as if they were functions.
1604 |     // If it is a function type, then validate parameters.
1605 |     if (childType.isFunctionType()) {
1606 |       FunctionType functionType = childType.toMaybeFunctionType();
1607 | 
1608 |       boolean isExtern = false;
1609 |       JSDocInfo functionJSDocInfo = functionType.getJSDocInfo();
1610 |       if( functionJSDocInfo != null  &&
1611 |           functionJSDocInfo.getAssociatedNode() != null) {
1612 |         isExtern = functionJSDocInfo.getAssociatedNode().isFromExterns();
1613 |       }
1614 | 
1615 |       // Non-native constructors should not be called directly
1616 |       // unless they specify a return type and are defined
1617 |       // in an extern.
1618 |       if (functionType.isConstructor() &&
1619 |           !functionType.isNativeObjectType() &&
1620 |           (functionType.getReturnType().isUnknownType() ||
1621 |            functionType.getReturnType().isVoidType() ||
1622 |            !isExtern)) {
1623 |         report(t, n, CONSTRUCTOR_NOT_CALLABLE, childType.toString());
1624 |       }
1625 | 
1626 |       // Functions with explicit 'this' types must be called in a GETPROP
1627 |       // or GETELEM.
1628 |       if (functionType.isOrdinaryFunction() &&
1629 |           !functionType.getTypeOfThis().isUnknownType() &&
1630 |           !functionType.getTypeOfThis().isNativeObjectType() &&
1631 |           !(child.isGetElem() ||
1632 |             child.isGetProp())) {
1633 |         report(t, n, EXPECTED_THIS_TYPE, functionType.toString());
1634 |       }
1635 | 
1636 |       visitParameterList(t, n, functionType);
1637 |       ensureTyped(t, n, functionType.getReturnType());
1638 |     } else {
1639 |       ensureTyped(t, n);
1640 |     }
1641 | 
1642 |     // TODO: Add something to check for calls of RegExp objects, which is not
1643 |     // supported by IE.  Either say something about the return type or warn
1644 |     // about the non-portability of the call or both.
1645 |   }

176 |   public static Node transformTree(AstRoot node,
177 |                                    StaticSourceFile sourceFile,
178 |                                    String sourceString,
179 |                                    Config config,
180 |                                    ErrorReporter errorReporter) {
181 |     IRFactory irFactory = new IRFactory(sourceString, sourceFile,

183 |     Node irNode = irFactory.transform(node);
184 | 
185 |     if (node.getComments() != null) {

===== M0826 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.AbstractScopeBuilder.resolveStubDeclarations()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 1668-1698
Documentation:
/**
     * Resolve any stub declarations to unknown types if we could not
     * find types for them during traversal.
     */
Method code:
1668 |     void resolveStubDeclarations() {
1669 |       for (StubDeclaration stub : stubDeclarations) {
1670 |         Node n = stub.node;
1671 |         Node parent = n.getParent();
1672 |         String qName = n.getQualifiedName();
1673 |         String propName = n.getLastChild().getString();
1674 |         String ownerName = stub.ownerName;
1675 |         boolean isExtern = stub.isExtern;
1676 | 
1677 |         if (scope.isDeclared(qName, false)) {
1678 |           continue;
1679 |         }
1680 | 
1681 |         // If we see a stub property, make sure to register this property
1682 |         // in the type registry.
1683 |         ObjectType ownerType = getObjectSlot(ownerName);
1684 |         ObjectType unknownType = typeRegistry.getNativeObjectType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
1685 |         defineSlot(n, parent, unknownType, true);
1686 | 
1687 |         if (ownerType != null &&
1688 |             (isExtern || ownerType.isFunctionPrototypeType())) {
1689 |           // If this is a stub for a prototype, just declare it
1690 |           // as an unknown type. These are seen often in externs.
1691 |           ownerType.defineInferredProperty(
1692 |               propName, unknownType, n);
1693 |         } else {
1694 |           typeRegistry.registerPropertyOnType(
1695 |               propName, ownerType == null ? unknownType : ownerType);
1696 |         }
1697 |       }
1698 |     }

   * equivalent to {@code (ObjectType) getPropertyType("prototype")}.

312 |   public ObjectType getPrototype() {

===== M0833 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.branchedFlowThrough(Node,FlowScope)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 134-246
Documentation:

Method code:
134 |   @Override
135 |   @SuppressWarnings("fallthrough")
136 |   List<FlowScope> branchedFlowThrough(Node source, FlowScope input) {
137 |     // NOTE(nicksantos): Right now, we just treat ON_EX edges like UNCOND
138 |     // edges. If we wanted to be perfect, we'd actually JOIN all the out
139 |     // lattices of this flow with the in lattice, and then make that the out
140 |     // lattice for the ON_EX edge. But it's probably to expensive to be
141 |     // worthwhile.
142 |     FlowScope output = flowThrough(source, input);
143 |     Node condition = null;
144 |     FlowScope conditionFlowScope = null;
145 |     BooleanOutcomePair conditionOutcomes = null;
146 | 
147 |     List<DiGraphEdge<Node, Branch>> branchEdges = getCfg().getOutEdges(source);
148 |     List<FlowScope> result = Lists.newArrayListWithCapacity(branchEdges.size());
149 |     for (DiGraphEdge<Node, Branch> branchEdge : branchEdges) {
150 |       Branch branch = branchEdge.getValue();
151 |       FlowScope newScope = output;
152 | 
153 |       switch (branch) {
154 |         case ON_TRUE:
155 |           if (NodeUtil.isForIn(source)) {
156 |             // item is assigned a property name, so its type should be string.
157 |             Node item = source.getFirstChild();
158 |             Node obj = item.getNext();
159 | 
160 |             FlowScope informed = traverse(obj, output.createChildFlowScope());
161 | 
162 |             if (item.isVar()) {
163 |               item = item.getFirstChild();
164 |             }
165 |             if (item.isName()) {
166 |               JSType iterKeyType = getNativeType(STRING_TYPE);
167 |               ObjectType objType = getJSType(obj).dereference();
168 |               JSType objIndexType = objType == null ?
169 |                   null : objType.getIndexType();
170 |               if (objIndexType != null && !objIndexType.isUnknownType()) {
171 |                 JSType narrowedKeyType =
172 |                     iterKeyType.getGreatestSubtype(objIndexType);
173 |                 if (!narrowedKeyType.isEmptyType()) {
174 |                   iterKeyType = narrowedKeyType;
175 |                 }
176 |               }
177 |               redeclareSimpleVar(informed, item, iterKeyType);
178 |             }
179 |             newScope = informed;
180 |             break;
181 |           }
182 | 
183 |           // FALL THROUGH
184 | 
185 |         case ON_FALSE:
186 |           if (condition == null) {
187 |             condition = NodeUtil.getConditionExpression(source);
188 |             if (condition == null && source.isCase()) {
189 |               condition = source;
190 | 
191 |               // conditionFlowScope is cached from previous iterations
192 |               // of the loop.
193 |               if (conditionFlowScope == null) {
194 |                 conditionFlowScope = traverse(
195 |                     condition.getFirstChild(), output.createChildFlowScope());
196 |               }
197 |             }
198 |           }
199 | 
200 |           if (condition != null) {
201 |             if (condition.isAnd() ||
202 |                 condition.isOr()) {
203 |               // When handling the short-circuiting binary operators,
204 |               // the outcome scope on true can be different than the outcome
205 |               // scope on false.
206 |               //
207 |               // TODO(nicksantos): The "right" way to do this is to
208 |               // carry the known outcome all the way through the
209 |               // recursive traversal, so that we can construct a
210 |               // different flow scope based on the outcome. However,
211 |               // this would require a bunch of code and a bunch of
212 |               // extra computation for an edge case. This seems to be
213 |               // a "good enough" approximation.
214 | 
215 |               // conditionOutcomes is cached from previous iterations
216 |               // of the loop.
217 |               if (conditionOutcomes == null) {
218 |                 conditionOutcomes = condition.isAnd() ?
219 |                     traverseAnd(condition, output.createChildFlowScope()) :
220 |                     traverseOr(condition, output.createChildFlowScope());
221 |               }
222 |               newScope =
223 |                   reverseInterpreter.getPreciserScopeKnowingConditionOutcome(
224 |                       condition,
225 |                       conditionOutcomes.getOutcomeFlowScope(
226 |                           condition.getType(), branch == Branch.ON_TRUE),
227 |                       branch == Branch.ON_TRUE);
228 |             } else {
229 |               // conditionFlowScope is cached from previous iterations
230 |               // of the loop.
231 |               if (conditionFlowScope == null) {
232 |                 conditionFlowScope =
233 |                     traverse(condition, output.createChildFlowScope());
234 |               }
235 |               newScope =
236 |                   reverseInterpreter.getPreciserScopeKnowingConditionOutcome(
237 |                       condition, conditionFlowScope, branch == Branch.ON_TRUE);
238 |             }
239 |           }
240 |           break;
241 |       }
242 | 
243 |       result.add(newScope.optimize());
244 |     }
245 |     return result;
246 |   }

===== M0860 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.ParserRunner.parse(StaticSourceFile,String,Config,ErrorReporter,Logger)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/ParserRunner.java
Original range: 104-147
Documentation:
/**
   * Parses the JavaScript text given by a reader.
   *
   * @param sourceString Source code from the file.
   * @param errorReporter An error.
   * @param logger A logger.
   * @return The AST of the given text.
   * @throws IOException
   */
Method code:
104 |   public static Node parse(StaticSourceFile sourceFile,
105 |                            String sourceString,
106 |                            Config config,
107 |                            ErrorReporter errorReporter,
108 |                            Logger logger) throws IOException {
109 |     Context cx = Context.enter();
110 |     cx.setErrorReporter(errorReporter);
111 |     cx.setLanguageVersion(Context.VERSION_1_5);
112 |     CompilerEnvirons compilerEnv = new CompilerEnvirons();
113 |     compilerEnv.initFromContext(cx);
114 |     compilerEnv.setRecordingComments(true);
115 |     compilerEnv.setRecordingLocalJsDocComments(true);
116 | 
117 |     // ES5 specifically allows trailing commas
118 |     compilerEnv.setWarnTrailingComma(
119 |         config.languageMode == LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT3);
120 | 
121 |     // Do our own identifier check for ECMASCRIPT 5
122 |     boolean acceptEs5 =
123 |         config.isIdeMode || config.languageMode != LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT3;
124 |     compilerEnv.setReservedKeywordAsIdentifier(acceptEs5);
125 | 
126 |     compilerEnv.setAllowMemberExprAsFunctionName(false);
127 |     compilerEnv.setIdeMode(config.isIdeMode);
128 |     compilerEnv.setRecoverFromErrors(config.isIdeMode);
129 | 
130 |     Parser p = new Parser(compilerEnv, errorReporter);
131 |     AstRoot astRoot = null;
132 |     try {
133 |       astRoot = p.parse(sourceString, sourceFile.getName(), 1);
134 |     } catch (EvaluatorException e) {
135 |       logger.info(
136 |           "Error parsing " + sourceFile.getName() + ": " + e.getMessage());
137 |     } finally {
138 |       Context.exit();
139 |     }
140 |     Node root = null;
141 |     if (astRoot != null) {
142 |       root = IRFactory.transformTree(
143 |           astRoot, sourceFile, sourceString, config, errorReporter);
144 |       root.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
145 |     }
146 |     return root;
147 |   }

Method code:
773 |   private void validateChildCount(Node n, int i) {
774 |     boolean valid = false;
775 |     if (i == 0) {
776 |       valid = !n.hasChildren();
777 |     } else if (i == 1) {
778 |       valid = n.hasOneChild();
779 |     } else {
780 |       valid = (n.getChildCount() == i);
781 |     }
782 |     if (!valid) {
783 |       violation(

===== M0872 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parse()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 182-951
Documentation:
/**
   * Parses a {@link JSDocInfo} object. This parsing method reads all tokens
   * returned by the {@link JsDocTokenStream#getJsDocToken()} method until the
   * {@link JsDocToken#EOC} is returned.
   *
   * @return {@code true} if JSDoc information was correctly parsed,
   *     {@code false} otherwise
   */
Method code:
182 |   boolean parse() {
183 |     int lineno;
184 |     int charno;
185 | 
186 |     // JSTypes are represented as Rhino AST nodes, and then resolved later.
187 |     JSTypeExpression type;
188 | 
189 |     state = State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION;
190 |     skipEOLs();
191 | 
192 |     JsDocToken token = next();
193 | 
194 |     List<ExtendedTypeInfo> extendedTypes = Lists.newArrayList();
195 | 
196 |     // Always record that we have a comment.
197 |     if (jsdocBuilder.shouldParseDocumentation()) {
198 |       ExtractionInfo blockInfo = extractBlockComment(token);
199 |       token = blockInfo.token;
200 |       if (!blockInfo.string.isEmpty()) {
201 |         jsdocBuilder.recordBlockDescription(blockInfo.string);
202 |       }
203 |     } else {
204 |       if (token != JsDocToken.ANNOTATION &&
205 |           token != JsDocToken.EOC) {
206 |         // Mark that there was a description, but don't bother marking
207 |         // what it was.
208 |         jsdocBuilder.recordBlockDescription("");
209 |       }
210 |     }
211 | 
212 |     // Parse the actual JsDoc.
213 |     retry: for (;;) {
214 |       switch (token) {
215 |         case ANNOTATION:
216 |           if (state == State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION) {
217 |             state = State.SEARCHING_NEWLINE;
218 |             lineno = stream.getLineno();
219 |             charno = stream.getCharno();
220 | 
221 |             String annotationName = stream.getString();
222 |             Annotation annotation = annotationNames.get(annotationName);
223 |             if (annotation == null) {
224 |               parser.addParserWarning("msg.bad.jsdoc.tag", annotationName,
225 |                   stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
226 |             } else {
227 |               // Mark the beginning of the annotation.
228 |               jsdocBuilder.markAnnotation(annotationName, lineno, charno);
229 | 
230 |               switch (annotation) {
231 |                 case AUTHOR:
232 |                   if (jsdocBuilder.shouldParseDocumentation()) {
233 |                     ExtractionInfo authorInfo = extractSingleLineBlock();
234 |                     String author = authorInfo.string;
235 | 
236 |                     if (author.length() == 0) {
237 |                       parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.authormissing",
238 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
239 |                     } else {
240 |                       jsdocBuilder.addAuthor(author);
241 |                     }
242 |                     token = authorInfo.token;
243 |                   } else {
244 |                     token = eatTokensUntilEOL(token);
245 |                   }
246 |                   continue retry;
247 | 
248 |                 case CONSISTENTIDGENERATOR:
249 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordConsistentIdGenerator()) {
250 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.consistidgen",
251 |                       stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
252 |                   }
253 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
254 |                   continue retry;
255 | 
256 |                 case CONSTANT:
257 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordConstancy()) {
258 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.const",
259 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
260 |                   }
261 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
262 |                   continue retry;
263 | 
264 |                 case CONSTRUCTOR:
265 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordConstructor()) {
266 |                     if (jsdocBuilder.isInterfaceRecorded()) {
267 |                       parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.interface.constructor",
268 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
269 |                     } else {
270 |                       parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.incompat.type",
271 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
272 |                     }
273 |                   }
274 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
275 |                   continue retry;
276 | 
277 |                 case DEPRECATED:
278 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordDeprecated()) {
279 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.deprecated",
280 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
281 |                   }
282 | 
283 |                   // Find the reason/description, if any.
284 |                   ExtractionInfo reasonInfo =
285 |                       extractMultilineTextualBlock(token);
286 | 
287 |                   String reason = reasonInfo.string;
288 | 
289 |                   if (reason.length() > 0) {
290 |                     jsdocBuilder.recordDeprecationReason(reason);
291 |                   }
292 | 
293 |                   token = reasonInfo.token;

932 |             state = State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION;
933 |           }

942 |             state = State.SEARCHING_NEWLINE;
943 |             token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
944 |             continue retry;
945 |           }
946 |       }
947 | 
948 |       // next token
949 |       token = next();
950 |     }
951 |   }

===== M0889 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.getPureBooleanValue(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 124-170
Documentation:
/**
   * Gets the boolean value of a node that represents a literal. This method
   * effectively emulates the <code>Boolean()</code> JavaScript cast function
   * except it return UNKNOWN for known values with side-effects, use
   * getExpressionBooleanValue if you don't care about side-effects.
   */
Method code:
124 |   static TernaryValue getPureBooleanValue(Node n) {
125 |     switch (n.getType()) {
126 |       case Token.STRING:
127 |         return TernaryValue.forBoolean(n.getString().length() > 0);
128 | 
129 |       case Token.NUMBER:
130 |         return TernaryValue.forBoolean(n.getDouble() != 0);
131 | 
132 |       case Token.NOT:
133 |         return getPureBooleanValue(n.getLastChild()).not();
134 | 
135 |       case Token.NULL:
136 |       case Token.FALSE:
137 |         return TernaryValue.FALSE;
138 | 
139 |       case Token.VOID:
140 |         if (!mayHaveSideEffects(n.getFirstChild())) {
141 |           return TernaryValue.FALSE;
142 |         }
143 |         break;
144 | 
145 |       case Token.NAME:
146 |         String name = n.getString();
147 |         if ("undefined".equals(name)
148 |             || "NaN".equals(name)) {
149 |           // We assume here that programs don't change the value of the keyword
150 |           // undefined to something other than the value undefined.
151 |           return TernaryValue.FALSE;
152 |         } else if ("Infinity".equals(name)) {
153 |           return TernaryValue.TRUE;
154 |         }
155 |         break;
156 | 
157 |       case Token.TRUE:
158 |       case Token.REGEXP:
159 |         return TernaryValue.TRUE;
160 | 
161 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
162 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
163 |         if (!mayHaveSideEffects(n)) {
164 |           return TernaryValue.TRUE;
165 |         }
166 |         break;
167 |     }
168 | 
169 |     return TernaryValue.UNKNOWN;
170 |   }

===== M0896 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CheckPathsBetweenNodes.checkAllPathsWithoutBackEdges(DiGraphNode<N, E>,DiGraphNode<N, E>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CheckPathsBetweenNodes.java
Original range: 163-193
Documentation:
/**
   * Verify that all non-looping paths from {@code a} to {@code b} pass
   * through at least one node where {@code nodePredicate} is true.
   */
Method code:
163 |   private boolean checkAllPathsWithoutBackEdges(DiGraphNode<N, E> a,
164 |       DiGraphNode<N, E> b) {
165 |     if (nodePredicate.apply(a.getValue()) &&
166 |         (inclusive || (a != start && a != end))) {
167 |       return true;
168 |     }
169 |     if (a == b) {
170 |       return false;
171 |     }
172 |     for (DiGraphEdge<N, E> e : a.getOutEdges()) {
173 |       // Once we visited that edge once, we no longer need to
174 |       // re-visit it again.
175 |       if (e.getAnnotation() == VISITED_EDGE) {
176 |         continue;
177 |       }
178 |       e.setAnnotation(VISITED_EDGE);
179 | 
180 |       if (ignoreEdge(e)) {
181 |         continue;
182 |       }
183 |       if (e.getAnnotation() == BACK_EDGE) {
184 |         continue;
185 |       }
186 | 
187 |       DiGraphNode<N, E> next = e.getDestination();
188 |       if (!checkAllPathsWithoutBackEdges(next, b)) {
189 |         return false;
190 |       }
191 |     }
192 |     return true;
193 |   }

===== M0907 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.prioritizeFromEntryNode(DiGraphNode<Node, Branch>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 196-215
Documentation:
/**
   * Given an entry node, find all the nodes reachable from that node
   * and prioritize them.
   */
Method code:
196 |   private void prioritizeFromEntryNode(DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> entry) {
197 |     PriorityQueue<DiGraphNode<Node, Branch>> worklist =
198 |         new PriorityQueue<DiGraphNode<Node, Branch>>(10, priorityComparator);
199 |     worklist.add(entry);
200 | 
201 |     while (!worklist.isEmpty()) {
202 |       DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> current = worklist.remove();
203 |       if (nodePriorities.containsKey(current)) {
204 |         continue;
205 |       }
206 | 
207 |       nodePriorities.put(current, ++priorityCounter);

===== M0916 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.getImpureBooleanValue(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 76-116
Documentation:
/**
   * Gets the boolean value of a node that represents a expression. This method
   * effectively emulates the <code>Boolean()</code> JavaScript cast function.
   * Note: unlike getBooleanValue this function does not return UNKNOWN
   * for expressions with side-effects.
   */
Method code:
76 |   static TernaryValue getImpureBooleanValue(Node n) {
77 |     switch (n.getType()) {
78 |       case Token.ASSIGN:
79 |       case Token.COMMA:
80 |         // For ASSIGN and COMMA the value is the value of the RHS.
81 |         return getImpureBooleanValue(n.getLastChild());
82 |       case Token.NOT:
83 |         TernaryValue value = getImpureBooleanValue(n.getLastChild());
84 |         return value.not();
85 |       case Token.AND: {
86 |         TernaryValue lhs = getImpureBooleanValue(n.getFirstChild());
87 |         TernaryValue rhs = getImpureBooleanValue(n.getLastChild());
88 |         return lhs.and(rhs);
89 |       }
90 |       case Token.OR:  {
91 |         TernaryValue lhs = getImpureBooleanValue(n.getFirstChild());
92 |         TernaryValue rhs = getImpureBooleanValue(n.getLastChild());
93 |         return lhs.or(rhs);
94 |       }
95 |       case Token.HOOK:  {
96 |         TernaryValue trueValue = getImpureBooleanValue(
97 |             n.getFirstChild().getNext());
98 |         TernaryValue falseValue = getImpureBooleanValue(n.getLastChild());
99 |         if (trueValue.equals(falseValue)) {
100 |           return trueValue;
101 |         } else {
102 |           return TernaryValue.UNKNOWN;
103 |         }
104 |       }
105 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
106 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
107 |         // ignoring side-effects
108 |         return TernaryValue.TRUE;
109 | 
110 |       case Token.VOID:
111 |         return TernaryValue.FALSE;
112 | 
113 |       default:
114 |         return getPureBooleanValue(n);
115 |     }
116 |   }

===== M0927 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.computeFollowNode(Node,Node,ControlFlowAnalysis)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 692-789
Documentation:
/**
   * Computes the follow() node of a given node and its parent. There is a side
   * effect when calling this function. If this function computed an edge that
   * exists a FINALLY, it'll attempt to connect the fromNode to the outer
   * FINALLY according to the finallyMap.
   *
   * @param fromNode The original source node since {@code node} is changed
   *        during recursion.
   * @param node The node that follow() should compute.
   */
Method code:
692 |   private static Node computeFollowNode(
693 |       Node fromNode, Node node, ControlFlowAnalysis cfa) {
694 |     /*
695 |      * This is the case where:
696 |      *
697 |      * 1. Parent is null implies that we are transferring control to the end of
698 |      * the script.
699 |      *
700 |      * 2. Parent is a function implies that we are transferring control back to
701 |      * the caller of the function.
702 |      *
703 |      * 3. If the node is a return statement, we should also transfer control
704 |      * back to the caller of the function.
705 |      *
706 |      * 4. If the node is root then we have reached the end of what we have been
707 |      * asked to traverse.
708 |      *
709 |      * In all cases we should transfer control to a "symbolic return" node.
710 |      * This will make life easier for DFAs.
711 |      */
712 |     Node parent = node.getParent();
713 |     if (parent == null || parent.isFunction() ||
714 |         (cfa != null && node == cfa.root)) {
715 |       return null;
716 |     }
717 | 
718 |     // If we are just before a IF/WHILE/DO/FOR:
719 |     switch (parent.getType()) {
720 |       // The follow() of any of the path from IF would be what follows IF.
721 |       case Token.IF:
722 |         return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
723 |       case Token.CASE:
724 |       case Token.DEFAULT_CASE:
725 |         // After the body of a CASE, the control goes to the body of the next
726 |         // case, without having to go to the case condition.
727 |         if (parent.getNext() != null) {
728 |           if (parent.getNext().isCase()) {
729 |             return parent.getNext().getFirstChild().getNext();
730 |           } else if (parent.getNext().isDefaultCase()) {
731 |             return parent.getNext().getFirstChild();
732 |           } else {
733 |             Preconditions.checkState(false, "Not reachable");
734 |           }
735 |         } else {
736 |           return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
737 |         }
738 |         break;
739 |       case Token.FOR:
740 |         if (NodeUtil.isForIn(parent)) {
741 |           return parent;
742 |         } else {
743 |           return parent.getFirstChild().getNext().getNext();
744 |         }
745 |       case Token.WHILE:
746 |       case Token.DO:
747 |         return parent;
748 |       case Token.TRY:
749 |         // If we are coming out of the TRY block...
750 |         if (parent.getFirstChild() == node) {
751 |           if (NodeUtil.hasFinally(parent)) { // and have FINALLY block.
752 |             return computeFallThrough(parent.getLastChild());
753 |           } else { // and have no FINALLY.
754 |             return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
755 |           }
756 |         // CATCH block.
757 |         } else if (NodeUtil.getCatchBlock(parent) == node){
758 |           if (NodeUtil.hasFinally(parent)) { // and have FINALLY block.
759 |             return computeFallThrough(node.getNext());
760 |           } else {
761 |             return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
762 |           }
763 |         // If we are coming out of the FINALLY block...
764 |         } else if (parent.getLastChild() == node){
765 |           if (cfa != null) {
766 |             for (Node finallyNode : cfa.finallyMap.get(parent)) {
767 |               cfa.createEdge(fromNode, Branch.UNCOND, finallyNode);
768 |             }
769 |           }
770 |           return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
771 |         }
772 |     }
773 | 
774 |     // Now that we are done with the special cases follow should be its
775 |     // immediate sibling, unless its sibling is a function
776 |     Node nextSibling = node.getNext();
777 | 
778 |     // Skip function declarations because control doesn't get pass into it.
779 |     while (nextSibling != null && nextSibling.isFunction()) {
780 |       nextSibling = nextSibling.getNext();
781 |     }
782 | 
783 |     if (nextSibling != null) {
784 |       return computeFallThrough(nextSibling);
785 |     } else {
786 |       // If there are no more siblings, control is transferred up the AST.
787 |       return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
788 |     }
789 |   }
