/** Find the l-value that the given r-value is being assigned to. */
Method code:
3040 |   static Node getBestLValue(Node n) {
3041 |     Node parent = n.getParent();
3042 |     boolean isFunctionDeclaration = isFunctionDeclaration(n);
3043 |     if (isFunctionDeclaration) {
3044 |       return n.getFirstChild();
3045 |     } else if (parent.isName()) {
3046 |       return parent;

===== M0952 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.LinkedFlowScope.allFlowSlots()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/LinkedFlowScope.java
Original range: 346-362
Documentation:
/**
   * Gets all the symbols that have been defined before this point
   * in the current flow. Does not return slots that have not changed during
   * the flow.
   *
   * For example, consider the code:
   * <code>
   * var x = 3;
   * function f() {
   *   var y = 5;
   *   y = 6; // FLOW POINT
   *   var z = y;
   *   return z;
   * }
   * </code>
   * A FlowScope at FLOW POINT will return a slot for y, but not
   * a slot for x or z.
   */
Method code:
346 |   private Map<String, StaticSlot<JSType>> allFlowSlots() {
347 |     Map<String, StaticSlot<JSType>> slots = Maps.newHashMap();
348 |     for (LinkedFlowSlot slot = lastSlot;
349 |          slot != null; slot = slot.parent) {
350 |       if (!slots.containsKey(slot.getName())) {
351 |         slots.put(slot.getName(), slot);
352 |       }
353 |     }
354 | 
355 |     for (Map.Entry<String, StaticSlot<JSType>> symbolEntry : cache.symbols.entrySet()) {
356 |       if (!slots.containsKey(symbolEntry.getKey())) {
357 |         slots.put(symbolEntry.getKey(), symbolEntry.getValue());
358 |       }
359 |     }
360 | 
361 |     return slots;
362 |   }

===== M0969 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.traverseName(Node,FlowScope)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 597-636
Documentation:

Method code:
597 |   private FlowScope traverseName(Node n, FlowScope scope) {
598 |     String varName = n.getString();
599 |     Node value = n.getFirstChild();
600 |     JSType type = n.getJSType();
601 |     if (value != null) {
602 |       scope = traverse(value, scope);
603 |       updateScopeForTypeChange(scope, n, n.getJSType() /* could be null */,
604 |           getJSType(value));
605 |       return scope;
606 |     } else {
607 |       StaticSlot<JSType> var = scope.getSlot(varName);
608 |       if (var != null) {
609 |         // There are two situations where we don't want to use type information
610 |         // from the scope, even if we have it.
611 | 
612 |         // 1) The var is escaped in a weird way, e.g.,
613 |         // function f() { var x = 3; function g() { x = null } (x); }
614 |         boolean isInferred = var.isTypeInferred();
615 |         boolean unflowable = isInferred &&
616 |             isUnflowable(syntacticScope.getVar(varName));
617 | 
618 |         // 2) We're reading type information from another scope for an
619 |         // inferred variable.
620 |         // var t = null; function f() { (t); }
621 |         boolean nonLocalInferredSlot =
622 |             isInferred &&
623 |             syntacticScope.getParent() != null &&
624 |             var == syntacticScope.getParent().getSlot(varName);
625 | 
626 |         if (!unflowable && !nonLocalInferredSlot) {
627 |           type = var.getType();
628 |           if (type == null) {
629 |             type = getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
630 |           }
631 |         }
632 |       }
633 |     }
634 |     n.setJSType(type);
635 |     return scope;
636 |   }

===== M0972 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.connectToPossibleExceptionHandler(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 830-857
Documentation:
/**
   * Connects cfgNode to the proper CATCH block if target subtree might throw
   * an exception. If there are FINALLY blocks reached before a CATCH, it will
   * make the corresponding entry in finallyMap.
   */
Method code:
830 |   private void connectToPossibleExceptionHandler(Node cfgNode, Node target) {
831 |     if (mayThrowException(target) && !exceptionHandler.isEmpty()) {
832 |       Node lastJump = cfgNode;
833 |       for (Node handler : exceptionHandler) {
834 |         if (handler.isFunction()) {
835 |           return;
836 |         }
837 |         Preconditions.checkState(handler.isTry());
838 |         Node catchBlock = NodeUtil.getCatchBlock(handler);
839 | 
840 |         if (!NodeUtil.hasCatchHandler(catchBlock)) { // No catch but a FINALLY.
841 |           if (lastJump == cfgNode) {
842 |             createEdge(cfgNode, Branch.ON_EX, handler.getLastChild());
843 |           } else {
844 |             finallyMap.put(lastJump, handler.getLastChild());
845 |           }
846 |         } else { // Has a catch.
847 |           if (lastJump == cfgNode) {
848 |             createEdge(cfgNode, Branch.ON_EX, catchBlock);
849 |             return;
850 |           } else {
851 |             finallyMap.put(lastJump, catchBlock);
852 |           }
853 |         }
854 |         lastJump = handler;
855 |       }
856 |     }
857 |   }

===== M0998 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.tightenTypesAfterAssertions(FlowScope,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 796-834
Documentation:

Method code:
796 |   private FlowScope tightenTypesAfterAssertions(FlowScope scope,
797 |       Node callNode) {
798 |     Node left = callNode.getFirstChild();
799 |     Node firstParam = left.getNext();
800 |     AssertionFunctionSpec assertionFunctionSpec =
801 |         assertionFunctionsMap.get(left.getQualifiedName());
802 |     if (assertionFunctionSpec == null || firstParam == null) {
803 |       return scope;
804 |     }
805 |     Node assertedNode = assertionFunctionSpec.getAssertedParam(firstParam);
806 |     if (assertedNode == null) {
807 |       return scope;
808 |     }
809 |     JSType assertedType = assertionFunctionSpec.getAssertedType(
810 |         callNode, registry);
811 |     String assertedNodeName = assertedNode.getQualifiedName();
812 | 
813 |     JSType narrowed;
814 |     // Handle assertions that enforce expressions evaluate to true.
815 |     if (assertedType == null) {
816 |       // Handle arbitrary expressions within the assert.
817 |       scope = reverseInterpreter.getPreciserScopeKnowingConditionOutcome(
818 |           assertedNode, scope, true);
819 |       // Build the result of the assertExpression
820 |       narrowed = getJSType(assertedNode).restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
821 |     } else {
822 |       // Handle assertions that enforce expressions are of a certain type.
823 |       JSType type = getJSType(assertedNode);
824 |       narrowed = type.getGreatestSubtype(assertedType);
825 |       if (assertedNodeName != null && type.differsFrom(narrowed)) {
826 |         scope = narrowScope(scope, assertedNode, narrowed);
827 |       }
828 |     }
829 | 
830 |     if (getJSType(callNode).differsFrom(narrowed)) {
831 |       callNode.setJSType(narrowed);
832 |     }
833 |     return scope;
834 |   }

===== M1010 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ComposeWarningsGuard.addGuard(WarningsGuard)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ComposeWarningsGuard.java
Original range: 87-102
Documentation:

Method code:
87 |   void addGuard(WarningsGuard guard) {
88 |     if (guard instanceof ComposeWarningsGuard) {
89 |       ComposeWarningsGuard composeGuard = (ComposeWarningsGuard) guard;
90 |       if (composeGuard.demoteErrors) {
91 |         this.demoteErrors = composeGuard.demoteErrors;
92 |       }
93 | 
94 |       // Reverse the guards, so that they have the same order in the result.
95 |       addGuards(Lists.newArrayList(composeGuard.guards.descendingSet()));
96 |     } else {
97 |       numberOfAdds++;
98 |       orderOfAddition.put(guard, numberOfAdds);
99 |       guards.remove(guard);
100 |       guards.add(guard);

===== M1018 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.hoistExterns(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 1367-1404
Documentation:
/**
   * Hoists inputs with the @externs annotation into the externs list.
   */
Method code:
1367 |   private void hoistExterns(Node externsRoot) {
1368 |     boolean staleInputs = false;
1369 |     for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1370 |       if (options.dependencyOptions.needsManagement() &&
1371 |           options.closurePass) {
1372 |         // If we're doing scanning dependency info anyway, use that
1373 |         // information to skip sources that obviously aren't externs.
1374 |         if (!input.getProvides().isEmpty() || !input.getRequires().isEmpty()) {
1375 |           continue;
1376 |         }
1377 |       }
1378 | 
1379 |       Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1380 | 
1381 |       // Inputs can have a null AST on a parse error.
1382 |       if (n == null) {
1383 |         continue;
1384 |       }
1385 | 
1386 |       JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
1387 |       if (info != null && info.isExterns()) {
1388 |         // If the input file is explicitly marked as an externs file, then
1389 |         // assume the programmer made a mistake and throw it into
1390 |         // the externs pile anyways.
1391 |         externsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1392 |         input.setIsExtern(true);
1393 | 
1394 |         input.getModule().remove(input);
1395 | 
1396 |         externs.add(input);
1397 |         staleInputs = true;
1398 |       }
1399 |     }
1400 | 
1401 |     if (staleInputs) {
1402 |       repartitionInputs();
1403 |     }
1404 |   }

===== M1021 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSTypeRegistry.createParameters(boolean,JSType...)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSTypeRegistry.java
Original range: 1246-1257
Documentation:
/**
   * Creates a tree hierarchy representing a typed argument list.
   *
   * @param lastVarArgs whether the last type should considered as a variable
   *     length argument.
   * @param parameterTypes the parameter types. The last element of this array
   *     is considered a variable length argument is {@code lastVarArgs} is
   *     {@code true}.
   * @return a tree hierarchy representing a typed argument list
   */
Method code:
1246 |   private Node createParameters(boolean lastVarArgs, JSType... parameterTypes) {
1247 |     FunctionParamBuilder builder = new FunctionParamBuilder(this);
1248 |     int max = parameterTypes.length - 1;
1249 |     for (int i = 0; i <= max; i++) {
1250 |       if (lastVarArgs && i == max) {
1251 |         builder.addVarArgs(parameterTypes[i]);
1252 |       } else {
1253 |         builder.addRequiredParams(parameterTypes[i]);
1254 |       }
1255 |     }
1256 |     return builder.build();
1257 |   }

===== M1027 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.visitAssign(NodeTraversal,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 839-941
Documentation:
/**
   * Visits an assignment <code>lvalue = rvalue</code>. If the
   * <code>lvalue</code> is a prototype modification, we change the schema
   * of the object type it is referring to.
   * @param t the traversal
   * @param assign the assign node
   * (<code>assign.isAssign()</code> is an implicit invariant)
   */
Method code:
839 |   private void visitAssign(NodeTraversal t, Node assign) {
840 |     JSDocInfo info = assign.getJSDocInfo();
841 |     Node lvalue = assign.getFirstChild();
842 |     Node rvalue = assign.getLastChild();
843 | 
844 |     // Check property sets to 'object.property' when 'object' is known.
845 |     if (lvalue.isGetProp()) {
846 |       Node object = lvalue.getFirstChild();
847 |       JSType objectJsType = getJSType(object);
848 |       String property = lvalue.getLastChild().getString();
849 | 
850 |       // the first name in this getprop refers to an interface
851 |       // we perform checks in addition to the ones below
852 |       if (object.isGetProp()) {
853 |         JSType jsType = getJSType(object.getFirstChild());
854 |         if (jsType.isInterface() &&
855 |             object.getLastChild().getString().equals("prototype")) {
856 |           visitInterfaceGetprop(t, assign, object, property, lvalue, rvalue);
857 |         }
858 |       }
859 | 
860 |       checkEnumAlias(t, info, rvalue);
861 | 
862 |       // Prototype assignments are special, because they actually affect
863 |       // the definition of a class. These are mostly validated
864 |       // during TypedScopeCreator, and we only look for the "dumb" cases here.
865 |       // object.prototype = ...;
866 |       if (property.equals("prototype")) {
867 |         if (objectJsType != null && objectJsType.isFunctionType()) {
868 |           FunctionType functionType = objectJsType.toMaybeFunctionType();
869 |           if (functionType.isConstructor()) {
870 |             JSType rvalueType = rvalue.getJSType();
871 |             validator.expectObject(t, rvalue, rvalueType,
872 |                 OVERRIDING_PROTOTYPE_WITH_NON_OBJECT);
873 |             return;
874 |           }
875 |         }
876 |       }
877 | 
878 |       // The generic checks for 'object.property' when 'object' is known,
879 |       // and 'property' is declared on it.
880 |       // object.property = ...;
881 |       ObjectType type = ObjectType.cast(
882 |           objectJsType.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined());
883 |       if (type != null) {
884 |         if (type.hasProperty(property) &&
885 |             !type.isPropertyTypeInferred(property) &&
886 |             !propertyIsImplicitCast(type, property)) {
887 |           JSType expectedType = type.getPropertyType(property);
888 |           if (!expectedType.isUnknownType()) {
889 |             validator.expectCanAssignToPropertyOf(
890 |                 t, assign, getJSType(rvalue),
891 |                 expectedType, object, property);
892 |             checkPropertyInheritanceOnGetpropAssign(
893 |                 t, assign, object, property, info, expectedType);
894 |             return;
895 |           }
896 |         }
897 |       }
898 | 
899 |       // If we couldn't get the property type with normal object property
900 |       // lookups, then check inheritance anyway with the unknown type.
901 |       checkPropertyInheritanceOnGetpropAssign(
902 |           t, assign, object, property, info, getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE));
903 |     }
904 | 
905 |     // Check qualified name sets to 'object' and 'object.property'.
906 |     // This can sometimes handle cases when the type of 'object' is not known.
907 |     // e.g.,
908 |     // var obj = createUnknownType();
909 |     // /** @type {number} */ obj.foo = true;
910 |     JSType leftType = getJSType(lvalue);
911 |     if (lvalue.isQualifiedName()) {
912 |       // variable with inferred type case
913 |       JSType rvalueType = getJSType(assign.getLastChild());
914 |       Var var = t.getScope().getVar(lvalue.getQualifiedName());
915 |       if (var != null) {
916 |         if (var.isTypeInferred()) {
917 |           return;
918 |         }
919 | 
920 |         if (NodeUtil.getRootOfQualifiedName(lvalue).isThis() &&
921 |             t.getScope() != var.getScope()) {
922 |           // Don't look at "this.foo" variables from other scopes.
923 |           return;
924 |         }
925 | 
926 |         if (var.getType() != null) {
927 |           leftType = var.getType();
928 |         }
929 |       }
930 |     }
931 | 
932 |     // Fall through case for arbitrary LHS and arbitrary RHS.
933 |     Node rightChild = assign.getLastChild();
934 |     JSType rightType = getJSType(rightChild);
935 |     if (validator.expectCanAssignTo(
936 |             t, assign, rightType, leftType, "assignment")) {
937 |       ensureTyped(t, assign, rightType);
938 |     } else {
939 |       ensureTyped(t, assign);
940 |     }
941 |   }
